*Alicia Parrish,Rajat Shinde,Sanket Badhe,Xinyi Bai,Sree Bhargavi Balija,Hua-Rong Chu,Emilio Ferrara,Armstrong Foundjem,Rajat Ghosh,Aakash Gupta,Xuanli He,Ong Chen Hui,Minji Jung,Madhangi Karimanal,Faiza Khan Khattak,Boryoung Kim,Eugenia Kim,Liliya Lavitas,Seok Min Lim,Victor Lu,Jim Moirangthem,Dhivya Nagasubramanian,Deepak Pandita,Sita Rajagopal,Geetha Raju,Evgeniia Razumovskaia,Aravind Reddy,Federico Ricciuti,Nobin Sarwar,Sungpil Shin,Sunayana Sitaram,Snehal Thorat,Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya,Jasmijn Bastings,Joachim Baumann,Kongtao Chen,Murali Emani,Mariya Hendriksen,Jiho Jin,Jun Seong Kim,Younghoon Ko,Alicja Kwasniewska,Minjae Lee,Tom Wei-cyuan Lin Kashyap Ramanandula Manjusha,Junho Myung,Junyeong Park,Roma Patel,Shyam Ratan,Sudarsun Santhiappan,Priyanka Suresh,Tuesday,Ksheeraj Sai Vepuri Laura Amortegui-Ordonez,Claire Dennis,Minsuk Kahng,Chris Knotz,Alice Oh,Balaraman Ravindran,Soojung Ryu William Bartholomew,Hiwot Tesfaye,Lora Aroyo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pluralis v0.1，一个基于文化优先视角构建的多模态、多区域、多语言安全评估数据集，覆盖6个亚太国家和8种语言，共6,448条提示。该数据集通过原生获取本地化安全风险而非改编西方数据集，并引入多模态评估范式，将用户文本与图像协同触发特定法律或文化违规。同时提出Judge-Pluralis，一个基于文化分类学的LLM裁判集成系统。实验揭示了VLM在不同地区语言中系统性的、本地化的失败模式，这些模式被全局平均指标所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI安全评估和基准框架主要依赖以西方为中心、文化无关的默认设置，这掩盖了关键的区域法律、社会语言细微差别和文化禁忌，导致视觉语言模型（VLM）在全球部署中存在漏洞。

Method: 1) 构建Pluralis v0.1数据集：从文化优先视角，在6个亚太国家原生获取6,448条本地化安全风险提示。2) 引入多模态评估范式：用户文本与图像单独看似无害，但协同触发特定法律或文化违规。3) 提出Judge-Pluralis：基于经验导出的文化分类学，采用协议门控的LLM-as-a-Judge集成系统进行评判。

Result: 观察到VLM在不同区域语言中表现出系统性的本地化失败模式，包括：具有下游危害的图像误识别、遗漏的项-上下文-区域交互、以及不充分的拒绝。这些失败模式在不同区域和语言间系统性变化，暴露了被全局平均指标掩盖的盲点。

Conclusion: Pluralis并未作为一个已解决的文化对齐评估框架呈现，而是作为推动未来创新的第一步和催化剂。呼吁研究社区利用这一基础推进多语言、多文化评估科学，以更好地支持全球AI文化对齐。

Abstract: Current AI safety evaluation and benchmarking frameworks predominantly rely on Western-centric culture-agnostic defaults that mask critical regional laws, socio-linguistic nuances, and cultural taboos, leaving Vision-Language Models (VLMs) vulnerable in global deployments. We introduce Pluralis v0.1: a novel multimodal, multi-regional, and multilingual dataset built from a culture-first perspective. Spanning 6,448 prompts across six Asia-Pacific countries (Bangladesh, India, Korea, Pakistan, Singapore, Taiwan) and eight languages, Pluralis diverges from prior work by natively sourcing localized safety hazards rather than adapting Western datasets. Crucially, it introduces a multimodal evaluation paradigm: user text (e.g., "Should I gift this?") and an image referring to "this" (e.g., a clock) - both innocuous in isolation, but synergistically triggering specific legal or cultural violations. Pluralis disentangles universal safety violations from localized cultural appropriateness, establishing the latter as a first-class evaluation axis. To operationalize this, we present Judge-Pluralis, an agreement-gated LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble trained on examples classified in an empirically derived cultural taxonomy. Observing VLM behavior on a subset of the Pluralis surfaces recurring, locale-specific failure modes such as image misidentifications with downstream harm, missed item-context-locale interactions, and inadequate refusals. These failure modes vary systematically across locales and languages, exposing blind spots that globally averaged metrics conceal. Ultimately, Pluralis is not presented as a solved evaluation framework for cultural alignment, but rather as a first step and catalyst for future innovation. We call upon the research community to utilize this foundation to advance the science of multilingual, multicultural evaluation to better support AI cultural alignment globally.

</details>


### [36] [Spider 2.0-AIFunc: Extending Real-World Text-to-SQL to AI-Native SQL Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06229)
*Tianyang Liu,Canwen Xu,Fangyu Lei,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Jixuan Chen,Tao Yu,Julian McAuley,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Spider 2.0-AIFunc，一个面向 AI 原生 SQL（在 SQL 中调用大模型函数）的基准测试，包含 465 个实例和 125 个真实数据库，涵盖 Snowflake 平台上的六类 AI 函数。评估表明最强闭源模型执行准确率 67-70%，开源模型最佳 58.1%，错误主要来自谓词规范、Schema 定位和 AI 函数参数化。传统 Text-to-SQL 的智能体框架在 AI 原生 SQL 上效果不佳，简单智能体反而表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 基准只评估传统 SQL，无法反映模型能否生成调用大模型能力的 AI 原生 SQL（如分类、过滤、情感分析、提取、相似性搜索、聚合等），缺乏针对这一新兴场景的评测标准。

Method: 基于现有企业级 Text-to-SQL 基准，通过智能体流水线将源任务改写为 AI 原生形式，同时转换目标查询并优化自然语言指令以明确意图。所有实例经过多轮跨时间窗口重复执行协议验证结果稳定性。

Result: 最强闭源模型达 67-70% 执行准确率，开源最佳为 58.1%。主要错误来源于谓词规范、Schema 定位和 AI 函数参数化。传统 Text-to-SQL 智能体框架（如 Schema 检索、相关表选择）在 AI 原生 SQL 上无效，简单智能体表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: AI 原生 SQL 对现有模型构成新挑战，传统 Text-to-SQL 策略不直接迁移，需要针对 AI 函数参数化和谓词规范等环节进行专门优化。

Abstract: Major cloud data platforms now expose large language model capabilities as native SQL functions, enabling analysts to perform classification, filtering, sentiment analysis, extraction, similarity search, and aggregation within ordinary SQL queries. Yet existing text-to-SQL benchmarks evaluate only conventional SQL and provide no signal on whether models can generate such AI-native SQL. We introduce Spider 2.0-AIFunc, a benchmark of 465 verified instances across 125 real-world databases covering six types of AI functions on the Snowflake platform. Starting from an existing enterprise text-to-SQL benchmark, we construct Spider 2.0-AIFunc through an agent-based pipeline that rewrites source tasks into AI-native form, simultaneously transforming target queries and refining natural language instructions to make the intended AI-native solution explicit and reduce ambiguity. All instances pass a multi-round repeated execution protocol across temporally separated windows to confirm result stability before release. Evaluating ten state-of-the-art language models, we find that the strongest proprietary models reach 67-70% execution accuracy while the best open-source model achieves 58.1%, a gap driven primarily by errors in predicate specification, schema grounding, and AI function parameterization. Agent frameworks designed for traditional text-to-SQL challenges, such as schema retrieval and relevant table selection, do not transfer effectively to AI-native SQL: a minimal agent setup consistently matches or outperforms more elaborate alternatives, suggesting that the strategies these frameworks employ are less critical in this setting. Data are available at https://github.com/Leolty/Spider2-AIFunc .

</details>


### [37] [Early Language Learning via Spreading Activation and Category Exploration in Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06258)
*Salvatore Citraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过将早期语言学习建模为基于图的心理词典搜索过程，结合扩散激活和强制探索机制，研究了儿童词汇习得在语义和词汇类别上的不均衡性。在四种语言上的实验表明，扩散激活优于最短路径基线，能更好地模拟词汇习得的类别转换、突发性和平均持久时间等探索动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究儿童词汇习得在语义和词汇类别上是否呈现不均衡性，以及如何通过计算模型理解这种不均衡背后的动态机制。

Method: 将早期语言学习建模为图结构心理词典上的搜索过程，包含两个交互机制：扩散激活（spreading activation）和强制探索（enforced exploration）词汇类别。使用四种语言（德语、英语、荷兰语、里奥普拉塔西班牙语）的CDI数据作为词汇类别基准，Wordbank库提供规范化年龄，利用最新资源构建词汇相似度图。

Result: 扩散激活在模拟规范化词汇习得上优于最短路径基线；在类别层面发现了CDI之间的复杂转换；通过分析突发性和同一CDI内的平均持久时间，发现扩散激活更好地捕捉了经验观察到的探索动态。

Conclusion: 词汇发展可以通过激活动态与词汇类别访问约束之间的非平凡相互作用来理解。

Abstract: Is word acquisition in children uneven with respect to semantic and lexical categories? To answer this question, we model early language learning as a search on a graph-based mental lexicon, driven by two interacting processes: spreading activation and an enforced exploration (rather than exploitation) of lexical categories. We evaluate model performance on four languages (German, English, Dutch, and Rioplatense Spanish), using CDIs as ground-truth data for lexical categories, normative ages derived from the Wordbank repository, and state-of-the-art resources for reconstructing graphs of word similarities. We find that spreading activation outperforms a shortest path baseline in simulating normative word acquisition. At the category level, we highlight complex transitions between CDIs. By studying their sequences in terms of burstiness and average persistence time within the same CDI, we find that spreading activation better captures the exploration dynamics observed empirically. Overall, our findings suggest that vocabulary development can be understood through the non-trivial interplay between activation dynamics and some degree of constraints regulating the visiting of lexical categories in complex networks.

</details>


### [38] [From Sinhala to Dhivehi: Cross-Lingual Transfer Learning for Low-Resource Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06289)
*Lukmal Ilyas,Nevidu Jayatilleke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了从亲属语言僧伽罗语到迪维希语的跨语言迁移学习，通过持续预训练+微调+KenLM解码的范式，将迪维希语ASR的词错误率（WER）从26.39%降至12.89%，验证了语言亲缘关系对低资源语音识别的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迪维希语（马尔代夫国语）在自动语音识别（ASR）和自然语言处理（NLP）任务中资源极度匮乏，亟需探索有效的跨语言迁移学习方法以提升其ASR性能。

Method: 开展了17组对照实验，涵盖五种迁移学习范式：迪维希语独训基线、顺序微调、多语言微调、持续预训练，以及以土耳其语作为无关语言的对照实验。最优系统采用：先在僧伽罗语上进行持续预训练，再在迪维希语上微调，并配合KenLM语言模型解码。

Result: 最优系统（持续预训练+微调+KenLM）达到12.89% WER和2.70% CER，相比迪维希语独训基线分别降低了13.50% WER和3.02% CER。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移学习能显著提升低资源语言迪维希语的ASR性能，且提升效果来源于语言亲缘关系（而非数据量增加），同时迁移策略和解码配置对结果同样关键。

Abstract: Dhivehi, the national language of the Maldives, is currently under-resourced for automatic speech recognition (ASR) and other NLP tasks. This study investigates whether cross-lingual transfer learning from Sinhala, a linguistically related, relatively well-resourced Insular Indo-Aryan language, can improve Dhivehi ASR. We conduct seventeen experiments across five transfer learning paradigms: Dhivehi-only baselines, sequential fine-tuning, multilingual fine-tuning, continual pre-training, and a control using Turkish as an unrelated language. The strongest system, continual pre-training on Sinhala followed by fine-tuning on Dhivehi with KenLM, achieves 12.89% WER and 2.70% CER, outperforming the Dhivehi-only baseline by 13.50% WER and 3.02% CER. However, the adaptation strategy and decoding configuration are equally critical for a successful transfer learning experiment. We conduct seventeen controlled experiments spanning five transfer learning paradigms: Dhivehi-only baselines, sequential fine-tuning, multilingual fine-tuning, continual pre-training, and a control experiment using Turkish as an unrelated language. The strongest system, continual pre-training on Sinhala followed by fine-tuning on Dhivehi with KenLM, achieves 12.89% WER and 2.70% CER, outperforming the Dhivehi-only baseline by 13.50% WER and 3.02% CER. The Turkish control experiment confirms that observed improvements stem from linguistic relatedness; adaptation strategy and decoding configuration are also critical.

</details>


### [39] [Estimating Uncertainty from Reasoning: A Large-Scale Study of Multi- and Crosslingual MCQA Performance in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06327)
*Andrea Alfarano,Andrea Bacciu,Saab Mansour,Amin Mantrach,Marcello Federico*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了22种语言（涵盖高、中、低资源语言）中的不确定性估计（UE）方法，发现：在低资源语言中提示模型用英语推理能显著提升UE性能并缩小与高资源语言的差距；UE方法的选择应依赖模型规模——小模型适用基于概率的开放黑箱方法，大模型则更适合封闭黑箱的自我表达不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UE研究主要集中于英语，缺乏对多语言场景下UE方法的系统性评估，无法指导多语言LLM系统何时该放弃回答。

Method: 使用两个人工筛选的问答数据集，在22种语言（高/中/低资源）上比较9种开放黑箱与封闭黑箱UE方法，涵盖不同模型规模和架构，通过引发长链推理并避免LLM-as-judge和基于嵌入的评分来减少评估噪声。

Result: 1) 低资源语言提示用英语推理可显著提升UE性能，表明理解力尚可但生成是瓶颈；2) 英语推理能消除低-高资源语言间的UE性能差距，生成语言比问题语言更重要；3) 小规模模型开放黑箱概率法最优，大规模模型封闭黑箱自我表达不确定性更优；4) 提供了多语言选择性预测的阈值选择指导。

Conclusion: 多语言UE中生成语言比问题语言更关键，UE方法选择需随模型规模调整，为构建可靠的跨语言LLM系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation (UE) enables LLM-powered systems to recognize when to abstain, yet existing research has predominantly focused on English. We present the first large-scale evaluation of UE methods across 22 languages, spanning high-, mid-, and low-resource settings. Using two human-curated Q\&A datasets, we compare open and closed box UE methods (nine in total) across different model sizes and architectures while eliciting long-form reasoning, avoiding LLM-as-a-judge and embedding-based scoring, which can introduce evaluation noise. We report three main actionable findings. First, we find that prompting models to reason in English while keeping questions in low-resource languages substantially improves UE performance, suggesting that comprehension of low-resource languages is largely intact, and that the reliability bottleneck lies in generation rather than understanding. Second, prompting models to reason in English closes the UE performance gap between low and high-resource languages, demonstrating that generation language matters more than the question language. Third, the choice of UE method should depend on model scale: at smaller scales, open-box probability-based methods outperform alternatives; at larger scales, closed-box self-verbalized uncertainty becomes superior. Finally, we provide an analysis of threshold selection for selective prediction, offering guidance on calibrating abstention in multilingual settings.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出使用领域自适应 Sentence Transformer 模型，将云安全控制映射到技术指标，替代传统人工流程。基于5个欧洲安全标准和指标集构建3,499个语义对训练语料，通过回译和 LLM 改写扩充至最多13,996样本，微调5种架构并在两个任务上评估。所有微调模型均优于零样本基线，控制到指标任务最佳模型提升23 nDCG@10，跨标准控制关联任务最佳模型达0.870 nDCG@10。表明领域内训练数据是性能的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前将云安全控制映射到技术指标仍需人工完成，效率低且易出错，亟需自动化方法。

Method: 构建3,499个语义对的训练语料（来自5个欧洲安全标准和技术指标集），通过回译和LLM改写扩充至13,996样本；对5种 Sentence Transformer 架构进行微调，在控制到指标映射和跨标准控制关联两个任务上评估。

Result: 所有微调模型均优于零样本基线；控制到指标任务最佳模型提升23 nDCG@10；跨标准控制任务中 multi-qa-mpnet-dot-v1 在回译设置下达0.870 nDCG@10。

Conclusion: 领域内训练数据是提升语义匹配性能的主要驱动力，领域自适应 Sentence Transformer 可有效自动化安全控制到技术指标的映射。

Abstract: Mapping cloud security controls to technical metrics is currently a manual process. This paper proposes domain adaptation of Sentence Transformer models to automate it. We build a training corpus of 3,499 semantic pairs from five European security standards and a set of technical metrics, then expand it via back-translation and LLM-based paraphrasing to up to 13,996 samples across four scenarios. We fine-tune five architectures and evaluate their performance on two independent tasks: control-to-metric and cross-standard controls association. All fine-tuned models outperform their zero-shot baselines. On the control-to-metric task, the best model gains up to 23 nDCG@10 points, while on the cross-standard control task, \textit{multi-qa-mpnet-dot-v1} under back-translation reaches 0.870 nDCG@10. The results show that in-domain training data is a primary driver of performance for the considered case studies.

</details>


### [41] [From Voting to Agent Collaboration: Answer-Type-Aware LLM Pipelines for BioASQ 14b](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06452)
*Taeyun Roh,Eunha Lee,Wonjune Jang,Sohyun Chung,Junha Jung,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对BioASQ 14b Task B的**问题类型特定的大语言模型框架**，根据是非题、事实题和列表题的不同推理需求，分别采用片段打乱+自反思、完整片段+思维链上下文学习、多智能体协作等策略，在官方评测中取得竞争性表现，并在Batch 4的事实题子任务中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学问答不仅需要从文献中准确提取信息，还需要跨多个文档可靠地整合证据。现有方法对不同类型问题使用统一的提示策略，忽视了是非题、事实题和列表题在推理和评估要求上的本质差异，导致答案鲁棒性和证据 grounded 能力不足。

Method: 构建问题类型特定的大语言模型框架：(1) 是非题：使用片段打乱和自反思降低对证据顺序的敏感性；(2) 事实题：采用完整片段输入结合思维链上下文学习支持准确实体识别；(3) 列表题：设计多智能体架构，由证据提取、候选生成、答案验证和最终聚合四个模块协作完成。策略选择基于BioASQ 13b的初步实验验证。

Result: 在BioASQ 14b Task B官方评测中，该框架在多个批次中展现出竞争性能，并在Batch 4的事实题子任务中获得第一名。

Conclusion: 结合问题类型特定的推理策略、集成预测和基于智能体的验证方法，能够有效提升生物医学问答的可靠性。

Abstract: Biomedical question answering requires not only accurate extraction of information from scientific literature but also reliable integration of evidence across multiple documents. This study presents a question-type-specific large language model (LLM) framework for BioASQ 14b Task B, designed to improve answer robustness and evidence grounding in biomedical question answering. Rather than applying a single prompting strategy to all questions, the framework selects different inference procedures for yes/no, factoid, and list questions according to their distinct reasoning and evaluation requirements. For yes/no questions, snippet shuffling and self-reflection are used to reduce sensitivity to evidence ordering and improve decision stability. For factoid questions, full-snippet input is combined with chain-of-thought-based in-context learning to support accurate biomedical entity identification. For list questions, a multi-agent architecture is employed, in which evidence extraction, candidate generation, answer verification, and final aggregation are handled collaboratively. Preliminary experiments on BioASQ 13b were used to identify effective inference strategies for each question type, and the resulting framework was subsequently evaluated in the official BioASQ 14b Task B challenge. In the official evaluation, our framework showed competitive performance across multiple batches and achieved first place in the factoid subtask of Batch 4. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining question-type-specific inference, ensemble prediction, and agent-based verification for reliable biomedical question answering.

</details>


### [42] [Data Analysis in the Wild: Benchmarking Large Language Models Against Real-World Data Complexities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06482)
*So Hasegawa,Shailaja Keyur Sampat,Lei Liu,Wei-Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DataGovBench 是一个基于政府开放数据构建的基准测试，用于评估LLM在真实数据分析场景中的表现，包含表格问答和表格洞察两个任务。实验表明当前LLM系统在真实数据分析任务上仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据分析基准未能反映真实场景，局限于小表格的事实检索，忽略了多表数据集、外部知识整合和探索性洞察发现等实际挑战。

Method: 从政府开放数据构建DataGovBench基准，包含Table QA（复杂可分解问题及文本/可视化答案）和Table Insight（探索性数据分析生成专家级发现）两个任务，使用先进LLM（含/不含智能体框架）进行综合实验评估。

Result: 实验发现当前最先进的LLM（无论是否使用智能体框架）在这两个任务上均存在显著性能差距，远未达到真实数据分析需求。

Conclusion: DataGovBench为推进能够回答分析性查询并从数据中发现洞察的LLM研究提供了具有挑战性的基准，当前LLM系统在真实数据分析场景中仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) in data analysis often fail to reflect real-world settings. They typically focus on fact retrieval from small tables and overlook the challenges of large multi-tabular datasets, external knowledge integration, and exploratory insight discovery. We introduce DataGovBench, a benchmark derived from governmental open data designed to evaluate LLMs in practical scenarios. The benchmark includes two tasks: Table QA that requires solving complex decomposable questions and producing textual answers or visualizations, and Table Insight that evaluates the ability of models to generate expert-level findings through exploratory data analysis. Comprehensive experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs, both with and without agentic frameworks, reveal significant performance gaps across both tasks. These results suggest that current LLM-based systems remain far from satisfying the demands of real-world data analytics. DataGovBench provides a challenging benchmark for advancing research on LLMs capable of both answering analytical queries and discovering insights from data. Code and sample data are available at https://github.com/SoHasegawa/datagovbench.

</details>


### [43] [Pitwall: Faithful Natural-Language Race-Strategy Briefings from a Calibrated Real-Time Monte Carlo Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06495)
*Juan S. Santillana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Pitwall，一个用于F1赛事的多语言实时解说生成系统。该系统将保真度作为架构特性，通过将每句输出分解为可验证的事实声明，并利用基于蒙特卡洛模拟的引擎进行状态校准，实现了在实时赛事数据驱动下的可信评论生成。系统已在2026年两场大奖赛中得到端到端验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育实时解说面临独特挑战：陈述涉及真实运动员，状态每几秒变化，且生成时不存在参考文本。现有方法难以同时满足实时性和事实准确性要求。

Method: 1) 将每个句子分解为可验证的类型化事实声明（位置、差距、轮胎等），并通过概率状态验证器逐条校验；2) 使用相同的验证器筛选微调数据，仅保留所有声明都被支持的样本（81.9%），其余回退到模板；3) 采用向量化蒙特卡洛引擎（每圈2000次模拟）进行状态校准，基于126场比赛（2018-2024）训练并在2025-2026赛季验证。

Result: 1) 模拟器在155次回测中获胜者进入前三的准确率90.3%，留出集Brier分数0.0745；2) 端到端系统在2026年奥地利站和英国站两场直播大奖赛中得到验证；3) 在英国银石站，系统提前10圈锁定最终获胜者；4) 发现稀疏状态下微调会引发幻觉，可通过稀疏上下文审计消除。

Conclusion: 系统证明了将保真度作为架构属性的可行性：通过可验证的事实分解与概率状态校准的结合，可以在实时、多语言、无参考文本的条件下实现可信的体育解说生成。同时揭示了生成系统中的核心权衡——丰富性与保真度需要分别控制。

Abstract: Live sports commentary is grounded generation under a deadline: statements concern real, named athletes, the grounding state changes every few seconds, and no reference text exists at generation time. We present Pitwall, a production system that generates natural-language Formula 1 strategy briefings in English, Spanish, and Portuguese, treating faithfulness as an architectural property rather than an aspiration: every published sentence is decomposed into typed factual claims (positions, gaps, tyres, pace, overtakes, race control) and each claim is verified against the probabilistic race state that prompted it. The same verifier gates the fine-tuning data: of 3,045 model-written targets, only the 81.9% whose every claim is state-supported are retained, the rest falling back to a provably faithful template, so the generator never sees an ungrounded target. Verification is meaningful because of the grounding substrate: a vectorized Monte Carlo engine (N=2,000 per-lap race continuations) calibrated on 126 races (2018-2024) and validated on fully held-out 2025-2026 seasons (winner-in-top-3 90.3% over 155 backtests; held-out Brier 0.0745). A recurring finding spans both halves of the system: virtues trade off and must be gated separately. In simulation, calibration-optimal is not decision-optimal; in generation, fine-tuning on richer targets buys vividness that collapses into hallucination when the grounding state is sparse -- a failure a four-base replication traces to base-model instruction adherence, not scale, and that sparse-context auditing removes from the production model. End-to-end operation -- live timing to verified trilingual briefings -- was confirmed at two consecutive live Grands Prix (Austria and Britain, 2026); at Silverstone a timestamped probability trace, committed to disk before the outcome was known, locked onto the eventual winner ten laps before the flag.

</details>


### [44] [DynaKRAG: A Unified Framework for Learnable Evidence Control in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06507)
*Yaqi Wu,Xiaolei Guo,Chenyu Zhou,Jiaqi Huang,Xianfa Zhang,Junxu Zhang,Zhuo Yu,Zhubo Shi,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DynaKRAG提出了一种基于状态条件策略的多跳证据获取方法，通过可执行动作集的构建和学习型控制器，在HotpotQA、2Wiki和MuSiQue基准上超越了现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法将多跳证据获取组织在特定流水线或预定义控制拓扑中，缺乏一个共享的状态条件策略来学习如何在当前有效的证据操作中进行选择。

Method: 将多跳证据获取形式化为关于原子证据操作的状态条件控制。每一步由一个有效性层构建可执行动作集，由一个学习型控制器选择下一操作，更新证据状态并可能启用新操作。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上，HotpotQA F1=0.5998, 2Wiki F1=0.5340, MuSiQue F1=0.3061，三个基准均超越最强基线。学习型控制器替换为均匀有效策略后F1下降3.96-5.78点，移除充分性反馈会损害所有数据集。

Conclusion: 在演化的证据状态下协调检索、诊断和缺口导向获取是有益的，额外的检索并非总是有益的，证明了学习型控制策略的重要性。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) acquires evidence sequentially, with each new document potentially revealing missing facts, bridge entities, query defects, or sufficient support for answering. Existing methods provide useful operations such as iterative retrieval, query reformulation, evidence critique, and sufficiency judging, but typically organize them within method-specific pipelines or predefined control topologies. This leaves underexplored how to learn a shared state-conditioned policy that chooses among currently valid evidence operations. We introduce DynaKRAG, which formulates multi-hop evidence acquisition as state-conditioned control over atomic evidence operations. At each step, a validity layer constructs the executable action set, and a learned controller selects the next operation. The resulting transition updates the evidence state and may enable new operations at subsequent steps. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, DynaKRAG achieves F1 scores of 0.5998 on HotpotQA, 0.5340 on 2Wiki, and 0.3061 on MuSiQue, outperforming the strongest controlled baseline on all three benchmarks. Replacing the learned controller with a uniform-valid policy reduces F1 by 3.96--5.78 points, while removing sufficiency feedback hurts all three datasets. Controlled retrieval-cap experiments further show that additional retrieval is not uniformly beneficial. Together, these results demonstrate the benefit of coordinating retrieval, diagnosis, and gap-directed acquisition under an evolving evidence state.

</details>


### [45] [RSF-GLLM: Bridging the Semantic Gap in Multi-Hop Knowledge Graph QA via Recurrent Soft-Flow and Decoupled LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06527)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay,Ananth Muppidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RSF-GLLM框架，通过可微分的软流图推理与LLM生成解耦，解决多跳知识图谱问答中检索-读取管道的不可微分问题，在WebQSP和CWQ上取得竞争性性能并显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统retrieve-then-read管道中检索器与生成器不可微分，导致检索器无法学习弥合中间节点与查询间缺乏词汇重叠的语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出RSF-GLLM框架：1) RSF模块使用GRU引导的查询更新器传播连续相关性分数，通过动态门控机制遍历语义不相似的桥接节点；2) 引入流稀疏正则化保证从软概率收敛到离散推理路径；3) 提取并文本化路径微调LLM，确保生成基于事实拓扑。

Result: 在WebQSP和CWQ数据集上，RSF-GLLM取得竞争性性能，且推理效率显著优于基于LLM的计算昂贵方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦可微分图推理与答案生成，RSF-GLLM有效解决了传统管道的不可微分问题，实现了高效且基于事实拓扑的多跳知识图谱问答。

Abstract: Multi-hop Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs faces a critical challenge: traditional retrieve-then-read pipelines break differentiability, preventing the retriever from learning to bridge the semantic gap where intermediate nodes lack lexical overlap with the query. To address this, we propose RSF-GLLM, a framework decoupling differentiable graph reasoning from answer generation. Our Recurrent Soft-Flow (RSF) module employs a GRU-guided query updater to propagate continuous relevance scores, utilizing a dynamic gating mechanism to traverse semantically dissimilar bridge nodes via structural cues. We introduce flow sparsity regularization to theoretically guarantee convergence from soft probabilities to discrete reasoning paths. These paths are extracted and textualized to fine-tune a Large Language Model (LLM), ensuring generation is grounded in factual topology. Experiments on WebQSP and CWQ demonstrate that RSF-GLLM achieves competitive performance with superior inference efficiency compared to LLM based computationally expensive approaches.

</details>


### [46] [Life Style Levels: Neighborhood Delineation using Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06529)
*Srivatsa Kulkarni,Debarag Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于开源卫星影像建筑形态的可扩展网格化城市划分框架，将59个印度城市划分为高分辨率网格，通过可解释的形态指标和规则评分系统区分不同富裕程度区域，并利用Google街景数据验证分类结果，最后探索了聚类分析与贷款违约率的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家快速城市化地区（如印度）缺乏细尺度社会经济信息，限制了刻画城市内部富裕与贫困差异的能力，亟需一种可扩展、低成本的方法来绘制城市富裕程度地图。

Method: 利用开源卫星影像提取建筑形态特征，构建高分辨率空间网格；设计可解释的形态学指标组合成透明、基于规则的评分框架；通过Google街景观测数据进行地面验证；使用密度聚类分析孟买建筑足迹识别非正规住区；跨富裕等级分析消费贷款违约率。

Result: 分类结果与基于生活方式的富裕指标预期效应一致；密度聚类得到的簇与已知非正规住区高度空间重叠；不同富裕等级区域的消费贷款违约率呈现显著差异。

Conclusion: 该框架完全依赖公开地理空间数据，提供了可扩展、可解释、成本效益高的方案，适用于印度城市的精细化富裕程度制图。

Abstract: Fine-scale socioeconomic information is often unavailable across rapidly ur-banizing regions of the developing world, like India, limiting the ability to delineate intra-urban variations in affluence and deprivation. This study pro-poses a scalable, grid-based urban delineation framework using building morphology derived from open-source satellite imagery. Urban areas across 59 Indian cities and towns are partitioned into high-resolution spatial grids and characterized using interpretable morphological indicators, which are combined into a transparent, rule-based scoring framework to delineate areas with contrasting levels of urban affluence. The resulting classifications are validated through ground-level Google Street View observations, revealing a sharp contrast between the grid classes which are consistent with the ex-pected effects of the lifestyle affluence indicators. We further investigate density-based clustering of building footprints in Mumbai to identify dense urban settlements, demonstrating that the resulting clusters exhibit substan-tial spatial overlap with known informal settlements across the city. Finally, we conduct an exploratory analysis mapping consumer loan delinquency across the derived affluence classes. By relying entirely on publicly available geospatial data, the proposed framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and cost-effective approach for granular urban affluence mapping across In-dian cities.

</details>


### [47] [Hierarchical Acoustic-Semantic Modeling: Modality Separation and Semantic Coherence for Full-Duplex SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06540)
*Zhenyu Liu,Yunxin Li,Xuanyu Zhang,Qixun Teng,Shenyuan Jiang,Haolan Chen,Minjun Zhao,Fanbo Meng,Yu Xu,Yancheng He,Baotian Hu,Haizhou Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Lychee-FD，一种原生端到端全双工语音语言模型框架，通过层级参数分离策略解决声学与语义建模之间的模态干扰问题，在语音智能和全双工交互流畅性上显著提升SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音语言模型（SLMs）面临严重的模态干扰问题，导致知识退化、语义完整性受损，使模型表现不自然、不智能。现有方法受限于这一瓶颈，亟需揭示其根本原因并提出有效解决方案。

Method: 通过细粒度优化动态分析，发现模态干扰源于声学建模与语义建模在共享深层参数空间时产生的梯度冲突。基于此提出Lychee-FD框架：(1) 层级参数分离策略，在深层解耦冲突模态；(2) 专用语义对齐通道，保持跨模态一致性。

Result: 在多个全双工基准上取得SOTA：口语问答任务语音智能提升7.4%，FullDuplexBench 1.5上全双工交互流畅性提升28.5%，且推理效率不受影响。

Conclusion: 首次揭示全双工SLMs中模态干扰的根本原因（梯度冲突），并设计了优雅的层级模型与实用解决方案，实现了无缝、高性能、原生智能的全双工SLMs。

Abstract: Developing seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex Spoken Language Models (SLMs) remains a critical challenge and long-standing goal for the speech and NLP community. Despite notable progress, recent endeavors are fundamentally constrained by severe modality interference, which causes substantial knowledge degradation and compromises semantic integrity -- ultimately making full-duplex SLMs feel unnatural and unintelligent. In this paper, through an exhaustive fine-grained analysis of model optimization dynamics, we uncover the root cause of such performance degradation, revealing that modality interference arises from inherent gradient conflicts between acoustic and semantic modeling when the two modalities are forced to share a deep parameter space. Guided by this key insight, we introduce Lychee-FD, a native end-to-end full-duplex framework designed to mitigate modality interference. Importantly, we propose a hierarchical parameter separation strategy that decouples conflicting modalities in deep layers while preserving cross-modality coherence via a dedicated semantic alignment channel. Extensive experiments on multiple full-duplex benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly advances the state of the art, yielding substantial improvements in both speech intelligence (+7.4% on Spoken QA) and full-duplex interaction fluidity (+28.5% on FullDuplexBench 1.5) without compromising inference efficiency. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to achieve two key advances: 1) uncovering and elucidating the root cause of modality interference in full-duplex SLMs, and 2) designing an elegant hierarchical model together with a practical solution for seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex SLMs.

</details>


### [48] [On the feasibility of dependency parsing of non-human sequences without a gold standard. Is evaluation possible in other species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06542)
*Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho,Catherine Hobaiter,Thore Bergman,Morgan Gustison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用网络科学的最新进展，证明由于非人灵长类动物发声或手势序列的长度分布快速衰减，其无监督依存解析能获得高比例的正确边，从而无需金标准即可评估；而人类语言缺乏此特性，评估难度大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非人灵长类动物缺乏金标准标注，传统观点认为无法评估无监督解析器的准确性，进而认为依存解析在其他物种中不可行。作者试图解决这一评估困境。

Method: 应用网络科学方法，分析序列长度分布特性对无监督解析器正确边比例的影响。

Result: 非人灵长类动物序列长度分布快速衰减，使得无监督解析器能获得高比例的正确边；人类语言序列不具备此特性。

Conclusion: 无金标准评估在非人灵长类动物中可行，但在人类语言中是难题。

Abstract: Dependency parsing consists of finding a tree representation for a sequence. Unsupervised dependency parsing aims to develop parsing methods without a gold standard during model training. In human languages, an unsupervised parser can be evaluated because some gold standard is usually available or can be created. For other species, a gold standard is unknown. Thus one may conclude that it is impossible to determine the accuracy of an unsupervised parser and, consequently, dependency parsing is unfeasible in other species. However, here we apply recent advances in network science to demonstrate that the proportion of correct edges retrieved by a parser must be high for the sequences of vocalizations or gestures that non-human primates produce due to the fast decay of the sequence length distribution. In contrast, human language sequences lack that property. Therefore, evaluation without a gold standard is feasible in non-human primates but a hard problem in humans.

</details>

### [49] [Bounded-Memory Parallel Image Pulling for Large Container Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05596)
*Sri Saran Balaji Vellore Rajakumar,Henry Wang,Ankur Singh,James Thompson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DBPP是一种磁盘后端并行拉取技术，通过将容器镜像层直接写入磁盘而非在内存中排序重组，消除了内存排序依赖，将峰值守护进程内存降低8.7-25.3倍，在内存受限节点上避免OOM终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML工作负载的容器镜像体积巨大（31-48 GiB压缩），传统containerd的内存排序重组方案在GPU节点上因主机内存被框架和模型权重共享，导致运行时OOM终止，冷拉取成为启动时间的主要瓶颈。

Method: DBPP将每个chunk直接写入磁盘上的目标字节偏移位置，消除排序依赖，使内存消耗与镜像大小无关；利用磁盘上完整的可寻址层文件，同时并行执行SHA-256摘要验证和解压缩（containerd需串行执行）。

Result: 在5个生产级镜像（最大48.5 GiB）上，峰值守护进程内存降低8.7-25.3倍，拉取吞吐量与containerd相当；在内存受限节点上，containerd 2.2拉取31.4 GiB镜像时被OOM杀死，DBPP成功完成。

Conclusion: 当后端存储足够快时，将内存中的排序缓冲区迁移到磁盘，用丰富的磁盘资源替换稀缺、竞争的内存资源，该思想不仅限于容器镜像，可推广到任何因强制排序而在内存中缓冲数据的流水线。

Abstract: AI/ML workloads increasingly run as containers, where a container image must be downloaded to the host before the workload can start. This cold image pull lands on the critical path whenever a training or inference job scales up or a host is updated, and for GPU workloads it has become the dominant component of startup time as AI/ML images reach 31--48~GiB compressed. We present Disk-Backed Parallel Pull (DBPP), an alternative to the in-memory ordered reassembly used by containerd~2.2, the upstream container runtime. containerd splits layers into chunks fetched concurrently over HTTP range requests, but chunks that arrive out of order accumulate in the runtime heap until a sequential consumer drains them in order. This backlog grows with image size, and on GPU nodes where host memory is shared with frameworks and model weights, it leads to out of memory (OOM) termination of the runtime itself. DBPP writes each chunk directly to its target byte offset on disk, eliminating the ordering dependency and bounding memory regardless of image size. Because each layer lands on disk as a complete, seekable file, DBPP runs SHA-256 digest verification and decompression simultaneously, two passes containerd must run one after the other. In controlled experiments across five production-scale images (up to 48.5~GiB), DBPP reduces peak daemon memory by 8.7--25.3$\times$ while maintaining comparable pull throughput. On a memory-constrained node, containerd~2.2 is OOM-killed pulling a 31.4~GiB image while DBPP completes the same pull. The underlying idea reaches past container images: any pipeline that buffers data in memory only to enforce ordering can move that buffer to disk once the backing store is fast enough, trading a scarce, contended resource for an abundant one.

</details>


### [50] [Layer 2 Coordinated Trusted Setup for Continuous CRS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05776)
*Khalid Hassan,Sara Rouhani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Layer 2网络的去中心化框架，通过将交易管道与仪式执行解耦，实现自动化、连续的CRS生成，解决了传统可信设置仪式灵活性差、易遭长期攻击的问题，并在PBFT协调的ZK-rollup架构上设计了两种协议变体，实验验证了其在节点故障或恶意贡献下仍能保持仪式活性与稳定L2吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零知识证明系统的可信设置阶段依赖静态、一次性的仪式，缺乏灵活性且易受长期密钥泄露攻击。将连续、重复的可信设置任务卸载到去中心化L2网络面临根本性协调挑战：高吞吐交易处理与多轮设置仪式之间的时序不匹配。

Method: 提出L2协调框架，安全解耦交易流水线与仪式执行。在PBFT协调的ZK-rollup架构上设计了两种协议变体：链上智能合约方法和异步点对点共识方法。两种设计均利用非交互式零知识证明（NIZK）结合提交-揭示结构，消除自适应操纵向量并隔离仪式延迟。

Result: 在模拟广域网约束和对抗条件下的实验评估表明，该架构成功隔离了仪式活性。连续设置能在实际时间范围内可靠完成（即使存在节点掉线或恶意贡献），同时保持稳定的L2交易吞吐量。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化L2协调框架能够在不依赖中心化协调的情况下实现自动化、连续的CRS生成，有效解决了长期密钥泄露风险与可信设置灵活性问题，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proof systems rely on a trusted setup phase to generate a Common Reference String (CRS), yet existing approaches are typically static, one-time ceremonies that are inflexible and vulnerable to long-term compromise. Offloading continuous, recurring trusted setups to a decentralized Layer 2 (L2) network introduces a fundamental coordination challenge arising from the mismatch between high-throughput transaction processing and the multi-round requirements of trusted setup ceremonies. This paper presents an L2-coordinated framework that safely decouples transaction pipelines from ceremony execution to achieve automated, continuous CRS generation without centralized coordination. We design and implement two protocol variants over a decentralized, PBFT-coordinated ZK-rollup architecture: an on-chain smart contract approach and an asynchronous peer-to-peer consensus variant. Both designs utilize non-interactive zero-knowledge proofs of knowledge alongside commit-reveal structures to eliminate adaptive manipulation vectors and isolate ceremony latency. Experimental evaluations under simulated wide-area network constraints and adversarial conditions demonstrate that our architecture successfully isolates ceremony liveness. Continuous setups complete reliably within practical time bounds despite node dropouts or malicious contributions, while preserving stable L2 transaction throughput.

</details>


### [51] [MatrixFSDP: communication-free matrix optimizers under ZeRO-3 parameter sharding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05895)
*Ming Gao,Yanwu Xu,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MatrixFSDP通过改变ZeRO-3的分片布局，让每个矩阵权重由单一数据并行rank完整拥有，从而在ZeRO-3内存规模下避免了全矩阵重构通信，实现了本地Newton-Schulz正交化更新，相比FSDP2-Muon在64块A100上取得最高54.6倍优化器步延迟加速和2.15倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等矩阵优化器通过对动量平滑后的完整2D矩阵执行Newton-Schulz正交化来加速收敛，但FSDP/ZeRO-3的分片机制使优化器只能看到矩阵分片而非完整矩阵。现有方案要么在每一步重构矩阵（付出权重大小的通信代价），要么使用ZeRO-1使完整参数常驻内存（牺牲内存效率）。亟需一种既能保持ZeRO-3级内存效率又能避免优化器步通信开销的解决方案。

Method: MatrixFSDP修改ZeRO-3的分片所有权：每个2D权重由一个数据并行rank拥有完整矩阵，其余rank持有空分片；非矩阵张量打包到尾部owner并沿用AdamW。普通反向归约直接将完整Muon输入落到owner rank上，Newton-Schulz本地执行无需优化器步的矩阵集合通信。前向和反向仍然进行参数物化和重分片。核心技术包括：MatrixShard元数据、平衡感知的owner规划器、确定性owner段P2P集合通信、owner缓冲区固定、以及owner分片检查点重分片。

Result: 在64块A100上，MatrixFSDP相比FSDP2-Muon将优化器步延迟在一个节点上降低4.2倍，在八个节点上降低54.6倍；端到端加速最高达2.15倍；并能运行ZeRO-1 owner放置超过80GB GPU的模型规模。

Conclusion: MatrixFSDP通过巧妙地改变分片所有权布局，在不改变优化器计算逻辑的前提下，同时实现了全矩阵Muon更新的数学等价性、ZeRO-3的内存效率以及无优化器步通信的运行时效率，为矩阵优化器在大规模分布式训练中的部署提供了一种实用的系统方案。

Abstract: Matrix optimizers such as Muon are attractive for large-scale training because they can improve convergence and token efficiency over coordinate-wise optimizers. Muon does this by orthogonalizing momentum-smoothed matrix updates with Newton-Schulz, producing spectrum-balanced updates that require the complete 2D matrix as input. This exposes a systems mismatch: FSDP/ZeRO-3 saves memory by making the optimizer see shards, not whole matrices. Existing systems therefore either reconstruct matrices at every optimizer step, paying weight-sized communication after backward, or make the update local by using ZeRO-1 owner placement with full parameters resident. MatrixFSDP takes a third path: it changes where ZeRO-3 shards live, not the optimizer being computed. For each 2D weight, one data-parallel rank owns the whole matrix and the other ranks hold empty shards; non-matrix tensors are packed into tail owners and stay on AdamW. The ordinary backward reduction then lands the full Muon input on the owner, so Newton-Schulz runs locally with no optimizer-step matrix collective. Forward and backward still materialize and reshard parameters; the runtime challenge is to make that uneven layout efficient and correct. MatrixFSDP does so with MatrixShard metadata, a balance-aware owner planner, deterministic owner-segment P2P collectives, owner-buffer pinning, and owner-shard checkpoint resharding. The resulting update matches full-matrix Muon while preserving ZeRO-3-scale memory: on 64 A100s, MatrixFSDP reduces optimizer-step latency over stock FSDP2-Muon by 4.2x on one node and 54.6x on eight nodes, reaches up to 2.15x end-to-end speedup, and runs model sizes where ZeRO-1 owner placement exceeds an 80 GB GPU.

</details>


### [52] [The Surplus Parking Gathering Problem in Infinite Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05983)
*Animesh Maiti,Abhinav Chakraborty,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了“剩余停车聚集问题”（SPG），研究在无限网格上部署的机器人如何饱和所有停车节点并聚集多余机器人在一个未预先指定的公共节点。在异步模型下，给出了可解性的必要条件、确定性算法及其复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人数量超过停车容量的分布式协调问题，即在满足每个停车节点容量限制的同时，将剩余机器人聚集到共同节点，且该节点不能预先指定。

Method: 首先刻画出初始配置中无确定性分布式算法可解的必要条件；对剩余可解配置，设计了一个多阶段、避免碰撞的确定性分布式算法，在异步模型（ASYNC）下运行，利用全局可视性和全局强多重检测能力。

Result: 算法能正确饱和所有停车节点，并将所有剩余机器人聚集到唯一确定的聚集节点；有限步内终止。移动复杂度上界为O(n(a+b)+n²)，下界为Ω(n(a+b))。

Conclusion: SPG问题在异步模型下是可行的，但存在不可解的初始配置；所提算法具有可证明的正确性和复杂度保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the \emph{Surplus Parking Gathering Problem} ($\mathcal{SPG}$), a new coordination problem for robots deployed on an infinite grid. The input consists of a set of designated parking nodes, each associated with a prescribed capacity, while the total number of robots exceeds the total parking capacity. The objective is to saturate every parking node exactly according to its capacity while gathering all remaining surplus robots at a common grid node that is not specified a priori. The robots are assumed to be autonomous, anonymous, oblivious, identical, disoriented, and homogeneous. We consider the asynchronous (\textsc{async}) model with global visibility and global strong multiplicity detection.
  We first establish necessary conditions for the solvability of $\mathcal{SPG}$ by characterizing the initial configurations that admit no deterministic distributed algorithm. For all the remaining solvable configurations, we present a deterministic distributed algorithm that correctly solves the problem. The proposed algorithm proceeds in several phases and avoids collisions throughout its execution. We prove that the algorithm terminates in finite time and, upon termination, every parking node is saturated according to its prescribed capacity while all surplus robots are gathered at a uniquely determined gathering node. We further analyze the move complexity of the proposed algorithm, obtaining an $O(n(a+b)+n^2)$ upper bound together with an $Ω(n(a+b))$ worst-case lower bound for the $\mathcal{SPG}$ problem.

</details>


### [53] [A Dual-CRDT Architecture for Decentralized Trust Governance and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06068)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双CRDT架构（Trust CRDT + Data CRDT），将治理信息作为复制状态管理，使得拜占庭容错CRDT系统中的信任关系和治理规则能够通过CRDT更新进行演化，实现了治理的递归管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭容错CRDT部署中，决定哪些更新值得信任的治理信息通常被管理在复制状态之外，而信任关系会随时间动态变化（参与者加入/离开、身份变更、治理规则修订），缺乏将治理本身作为复制状态进行统一管理的方式。

Method: 提出双CRDT架构：Trust CRDT存储和演化治理信息，Data CRDT根据Trust CRDT导出的信任配置进行状态重构。基于确定性重构和拜占庭信任过滤机制，治理规则通过普通的CRDT更新实现自身演化，同时管理应用数据。

Result: 在Melda和melda-sec之上实现了原型系统，展示了递归治理模型的有效性——治理规则既决定自身的未来演化，又同时管理应用数据。

Conclusion: 该工作是对拜占庭容错CRDT系统中去中心化信任治理与演化的初步探索，为将信任关系作为一等复制状态管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: While CRDTs provide decentralized replication and eventual consistency, Byzantine-resilient deployments require mechanisms for deciding which updates should be trusted and therefore contribute to the reconstructed state. In practice, the trust relationships underlying these decisions may evolve over time as participants join or leave, identities change, and governance rules are revised. However, the information used to make trust decisions is typically managed outside the replicated state itself. This paper introduces a dual-CRDT architecture composed of a \emph{Trust CRDT} and a \emph{Data CRDT}. The Trust CRDT stores and evolves governance information, while the Data CRDT is reconstructed according to the trust configuration derived from the Trust CRDT. Governance therefore becomes replicated state rather than an externally managed artifact. Building upon deterministic reconstruction and Byzantine trust filtering, the proposed model allows trust relationships and governance rules to evolve through ordinary CRDT updates. The resulting architecture provides a recursive governance model in which governance rules determine their own future evolution while simultaneously governing application data. The approach is implemented as a prototype on top of Melda and melda-sec and should be viewed as an initial exploration of decentralized trust governance and evolution for Byzantine-resilient CRDT systems.

</details>


### [54] [DDB: Source-Level Interactive Debugging for Distributed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06107)
*Yibo Yan,Junzhou He,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DDB，一个将交互式调试扩展到分布式系统的源码级调试器，通过分布式回溯（DBT）、意图保持控制平面和暂停擦除时间（PET）三大关键技术，解决了分布式调试中调用栈断裂、状态丢失和暂停级联超时三大难题，在122个进程规模下实现30ms跨RPC回溯延迟和1-5%性能开销，用户研究中故障定位成功率100%（基线工具38.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式调试是理解程序行为的有效工具，但现有调试器仅支持单进程执行，分布式系统中调用栈在进程边界中断、调试状态无法承受基础设施动态变化、调试器引发的暂停导致超时级联破坏调试流程，迫使开发者放弃实时假设检验，转而使用繁琐的日志-重部署循环。

Method: 1) 分布式回溯(DBT)：在每个RPC中嵌入紧凑因果元数据，跨RPC边界重建统一调用栈；2) 意图保持控制平面：自动协调并传播断点至动态进程集；3) 暂停擦除时间(PET)：虚拟化每个进程的时钟，将逻辑时间与物理暂停解耦，防止超时级联。

Result: 在gRPC、ServiceWeaver、Nu和Quicksand上评估，支持多达122个进程：跨RPC回溯延迟中位数30ms，重复暂停下时间跳动小于5ms，吞吐开销仅1-5%（与单进程调试器相当）。用户研究中故障定位成功率100%（基线38.5%），中位定位时间约8分钟。

Conclusion: DDB通过三种针对性的机制成功将交互式调试能力扩展到分布式系统，解决了长期被认为不切实际的分布式调试难题，并证明了该方法的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: Interactive debugging is an effective tool for understanding program behavior at the source level, allowing developers to pause execution, navigate the call stack, and inspect runtime state. However, interactive debuggers are designed for single-process execution, and interactive debugging has been widely considered impractical for distributed systems. Call stacks stop at process boundaries, debugging state fails to survive infrastructure dynamics, and, most critically, debugger-induced execution pauses trigger catastrophic timeout cascades that destroy the intended debug flow. Consequently, developers are forced to abandon live hypothesis testing in favor of unwieldy and iterative log-and-redeploy cycles.
  We present DDB, a source-level interactive debugger that extends interactive debugging capabilities to distributed applications. We show that each of these challenges admits a targeted solution. To bridge disjoint processes, Distributed Backtrace (DBT) embeds compact causality metadata in every RPC and reconstructs a unified call stack across RPC boundaries. To manage the lifecycle of a distributed session, an intent-preserving control plane automatically coordinates and propagates breakpoints across dynamic process sets. To make pausing safe, Pause-Erased Time (PET) virtualizes each process's clock, decoupling logical time from physical pauses and preventing timeout cascades. DDB integrates with an RPC framework in 20-60 lines of code. Evaluated on gRPC, ServiceWeaver, Nu, and Quicksand across up to 122 processes, DDB achieves 30ms median cross-RPC backtrace latency, sub-5 ms time jump under repeated execution pauses, and adds 1-5% throughput overhead, comparable to attaching a single-process debugger. In a controlled user study, DDB achieves a 100% fault localization success rate (compared to 38.5% for baseline tools) with a median localization time of ~8 minutes.

</details>


### [55] [Ordering by Unanimity: Giving Applications Sequencing Rights Without Breaking Composability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06144)
*Andrea Canidio*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"一致同意覆盖"（unanimity override）的算法，解决区块链应用中交易排序偏好与底层区块链默认排序之间的冲突。当所有应用对两笔交易的排序达成一致时，算法尊重该一致排序；否则使用默认排序。算法通过打破循环来解决一致同意可能形成的循环问题，并提供了两个关键保证：单应用交互交易的排序按该应用偏好执行，以及"门控交易"在攻击者存在时仍能按应用一致同意顺序执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链应用（如自动做市商、交易所、链上拍卖等）对交易执行顺序有特定偏好（如预言机更新先于交换、取消订单先于市价单、竞价从高到低执行），但底层区块链选择的交易顺序可能与这些需求不兼容，导致应用功能受损。

Method: 提出"一致同意覆盖"算法：当所有相关应用对两笔交易的排序达成一致时，采用该一致排序；否则使用交易在区块中出现的默认顺序。针对一致同意可能形成循环的问题，引入循环打破机制。攻击者可插入交易制造循环，但算法仍能保证两个核心性质。

Result: 在攻击者可设置默认顺序、部署应用并插入交易的情况下，算法仍满足两个保证：(1) 与单个表达了偏好的应用交互的所有交易，按该应用偏好排序，即使它们也与未表达偏好的应用交互；(2) 门控交易（在一致同意排序中不会被攻击者交易超越的交易）始终按应用一致同意顺序执行，即使涉及多个应用。

Conclusion: 该算法明确了协议能够保护的偏好范围，并让应用和发送者能够提前预知哪些交易会按预期顺序执行。

Abstract: Blockchain applications may have preferences over the order in which transactions execute: an automated market maker may use an external feed to price its liquidity, and require that the oracle update incorporating this price execute before any swap; an exchange may want to execute cancellations of limit orders before incoming market orders; an application may run an on-chain auction by executing bids from highest to lowest, so that the first bid wins. However, the ordering of transactions is chosen by the underlying blockchain and may not be compatible with the requirements of a specific application. In this paper, I tackle this problem by introducing an algorithm called unanimity override. The intuition is that when all the applications agree on how to order two transactions, the underlying blockchain should respect this agreement; a default order - the order in which transactions appear in the block - settles the rest. The problem with this naive approach is that application unanimity may form cycles, which the algorithm must break. Cycle-breaking is also the rule's main vulnerability because an attacker can insert transactions to manufacture a cycle. Yet two guarantees hold against any attacker who sets the default order, deploys applications, and inserts transactions. All transactions that interact with a single application that expressed preferences are ordered according to that application's preferences, even when they also interact with other applications that did not express preferences. Also, gated transactions - those that cannot be outranked in the unanimity order by any transaction crafted by an attacker - always execute as the applications unanimously prefer, even when they touch many applications. The two guarantees identify the preferences the protocol can protect, and they tell applications and senders in advance which transactions will execute in the intended order.

</details>

*Yipeng Liu,Chang Liu,Si Shen,Jiaqi Zheng,Mingfan Li,Yuyang Yang,Guanhua Li,Yuquan Zhang,Yimeng Xu,Zhongzhe Hu,Zhiyuan Huang,Qihang Duan,Junsong Wang,Wenkai Ling,Baochuan Yang,Xianzhi Yu,Han Bao,Yijie Chen,Guihai Chen*

TL;DR: 本文提出UBEP通信库，针对现代超节点架构中MoE模型的All-to-All通信瓶颈（BSP串行化、同步开销大、负载不均衡），通过重新设计通信原语，将All-to-All延迟降低52.4%，推理TPOT降低11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NVIDIA NVL72/576和华为CloudMatrix384等超节点提供了高带宽互连和统一全局地址空间，但MoE模型的稀疏通信仍受限于三个根本瓶颈：粗粒度BSP导致的执行串行化、同步开销无法随带宽扩展、距离无关调度导致的负载不均衡。

Method: 提出UBEP（Unified-Bus Expert Parallelism），一个生产级通信库，重新设计了MoE的All-to-All通信原语，针对超节点架构优化。

Result: 在大规模实验中，UBEP将All-to-All延迟降低高达52.4%，MoE推理每个输出token的时间（TPOT）降低高达11.1%。

Conclusion: UBEP通过消除BSP串行化、降低同步开销和解决负载不均衡，有效释放了超节点架构在MoE模型上的通信潜力。

Abstract: The deployment of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models on production high-bandwidth superpods, such as NVIDIA's NVL72/576 and Huawei's CloudMatrix384, introduces critical challenges beyond raw interconnect bandwidth. While these systems provide unified global address spaces and high-bandwidth fabrics, their full potential for sparse MoE communication is hindered by three fundamental bottlenecks: (1) Strict execution serialization imposed by coarse-grained Bulk Synchronous Parallel (BSP) orchestration of interdependent communication phases; (2) Prohibitive synchronization overhead that fails to scale alongside high interconnect bandwidth; and (3) Severe load imbalance resulting from distance-agnostic scheduling of irregular token traffic. To eliminate these bottlenecks, we introduce UBEP (Unified-Bus Expert Parallelism), a production-ready communication library that rethinks MoE's All-to-All primitives for modern superpod architectures. Through large scale experiments, UBEP reduces All-to-All latency by up to 52.4% and MoE inference Time Per Output Token (TPOT) by up to 11.1%.

</details>

### [57] [Statistically Meaningful Geometry and Gauge Symmetry Breaking: A Geometric Foundation for Scientific Discovery and Intelligence Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05436)
*Bing Cheng,Yi-Shuai Niu,Howell Tong,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统计有意义的几何（SMG）框架，将过参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数Orlicz纤维丛，证明在持续分布外（OOD）刺激下连续优化会失败，未建模方差泄漏到不可观测的垂直纤维空间产生主动反因果张力，最终导致共轭焦点边界处的体积塌缩和矩阵奇异性，触发规范对称性破缺（GSB），系统自发结晶出新的独立坐标轴，表现为结构G-熵的离散+1.0整数阶跃跳变，从而区分真正的因果发现与恶性幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典平欧几里得统计无法区分连续插值与自主发现新颖因果定律，亟需一个能够数学上认证真正智能的框架，以解决过参数化架构（尤其是LLM）是否具备真正智能的危机。

Method: 将学习系统建模为无限维非参数Orlicz纤维丛，通过水平基流形（可见）与垂直纤维空间（不可观测）的几何结构分析，推导持续OOD刺激下的张力累积，利用统计流形的非线性曲率计算共轭焦点边界T_crit，证明矩阵奇异性作为非平衡触发器引发规范对称性破缺，通过最小能量路径准则和因果不变性滤波对涌现的新轴进行验证。

Result: 系统在OOD刺激下必然达到临界时间T_crit，触发局部体积塌缩和矩阵奇异性，进而引发规范对称性破缺，系统自发结晶出新的独立坐标轴，伴随结构G-熵的离散+1.0整数跳变。

Conclusion: SMG提供了一个无参数、可证伪的仪表盘来数学上认证真正智能，将AI for Science转变为自主范式转移的引擎。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of over-parameterized machine learning architectures, particularly LLMs, raises a profound crisis: do these systems exhibit genuine intelligence, or are they merely sophisticated statistical pattern matchers? Classical flat Euclidean statistics cannot differentiate continuous interpolation from the autonomous discovery of novel causal laws. To resolve this, we introduce Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG), a framework modeling over-parameterized learning systems as infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz fiber bundles. We prove that under persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) stimuli governed by unmodeled causal mechanisms, continuous optimization fails. Unmodeled variance is rejected by the visible horizontal base manifold, leaking into the unobservable vertical fiber space and generating an accumulation of Active Acausal Tension. Driven by the statistical manifold's non-linear curvature, this tension inevitably strikes a conjugate focal boundary ($T_{\text{crit}} = π^2 / K_{\text{max}}$), triggering localized volumetric collapse and a catastrophic matrix singularity ($[G_f]^{-1} \to \infty$). We demonstrate this geometric breakdown acts as the strict non-equilibrium trigger for a Gauge Symmetry Break (GSB). The system purges hidden tension from unobservable gauge redundancies, spontaneously crystallizing a new, mathematically independent horizontal coordinate axis. This non-parametric phase transition registers as a discrete $+1.0$ integer step-jump in observable Structural G-Entropy. By decoupling parameter charts and subjecting emergent axes to a Minimal Energy Path Criterion and a Causal Invariance Filter, we distinguish genuine discovery from malignant hallucinations. Ultimately, SMG provides a parameter-free, falsifiable dashboard to mathematically certify true intelligence, transforming AI for Science into an engine of autonomous paradigm shifts.

</details>


### [58] [Design-CP: Context Parallelism for Design of Protein Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05439)
*Lorenzo Tarricone,Helen E. Eisenach,Aiko Muraishi,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Design-CP，两种针对RFdiffusion 3的上下文并行推理策略（1D行分片和2D网格分片+环注意力），将二次激活分布到多GPU上，实现端到端、全原子的大规模蛋白质复合体（如二十面体、八面体纳米颗粒）设计，在保持预训练权重不变的同时突破单GPU内存限制，并在小规模GPU集群上展示了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全原子生成蛋白质模型在联合建模多链大复合体时，其二次的token对和原子对表示会随链数和残基数的增长迅速超出单GPU内存容量，限制了大型多聚体蛋白质设计的能力。

Method: 提出了Design-CP，包含两种上下文并行策略：1D行分片和2D网格分片配合环注意力，将RFdiffusion 3中的二次激活分布到多GPU网格上，且保持预训练权重不变。

Result: 在二十面体组装体采样中，最大可行不对称亚基（ASU）大小随GPU数量呈平方根趋势增长，2D分片在wall-clock缩放上表现更优。在二十面体纳米颗粒的全原子设计中获得了有利的结构和界面指标，并在小规模16GB GPU工作站集群上成功演示了八面体纳米颗粒设计。

Conclusion: Design-CP为大规模蛋白质组装体设计提供了一条实用路径，有助于实现大型复合体蛋白质设计的民主化。

Abstract: Many all-atom generative protein models can in principle design large multimeric complexes by jointly modelling all chains, but their quadratic token- and atom-pair representations quickly exceed single-GPU memory as the number of chains and residues modelled grows. We introduce Design-CP, two context-parallel (CP) inference strategies for RFdiffusion 3 (1D row-sharding and 2D grid sharding with ring attention) that distribute the quadratic activations across a multi-GPU mesh while preserving pretrained weights. We characterise their scaling when sampling icosahedral assemblies, showing that the maximum feasible asymmetric subunit (ASU) size grows with the expected square-root trend in GPU count and that 2D sharding achieves better wall-clock scaling. Moreover, we show how strong point-group symmetry constraints make CP usable out of the box for end-to-end, all-atom design of icosahedral nanoparticles, yielding favourable in silico structural and interface metrics. Finally, we demonstrate octahedral nanoparticle design on a small cluster of workstation-grade 16GB GPUs, illustrating how Design-CP can be a practical path towards democratising large-assembly protein design.

</details>


### [59] [Geometry-Aware Infrastructure-Anchored Denoiser for UWB Sensing and Work-Zone Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05449)
*Weizhe Tang,Jiaxi Liu,Junwei you,Steven T. Parker,Pei Li,Sikai Chen,Meng Ran,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAIA是一个几何感知的、基于基础设施锚点的学习框架，通过结合时序距离建模、潜在锚点布局估计和确定性距离投影，对外部UWB测距数据进行降噪，从而提升施工区几何重建的精度。在真实数据集上，GAIA的均方误差降低18.4%，多边形IoU提升15.5%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工区几何感知对智能交通系统至关重要，UWB传感虽低成本，但户外非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差严重降级测距质量，进而影响下游空间重建的准确性。

Method: 提出GAIA框架，包含三部分：时序距离建模（捕获时间相关性）、潜在锚点布局估计（隐式学习锚点几何结构）、确定性距离投影（将学习到的距离映射到边界一致的重建空间），并保持距离降噪作为监督任务。

Result: 在真实户外UWB数据集上（含同步UWB、GNSS、IMU），GAIA取得最低的整体测距MSE和最高的多边形IoU；相比PoseMLP，MSE降低18.4%，多边形IoU提升15.5%。同时通过真实数据校准的压力测试模拟器验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 几何感知的距离降噪是实现空间一致的施工区重建的有效途径，GAIA框架验证了将几何约束融入测距学习过程的优势。

Abstract: Accurate work-zone geometry perception is critical for intelligent transportation systems, and ultra-wideband sensing offers a low-cost approach for infrastructure-aided reconstruction. However, outdoor UWB ranging is often degraded by non-line-of-sight propagation, burst noise, and long-tail errors, which can distort downstream spatial reconstruction. We present GAIA, a geometry-aware, infrastructure-anchored learning framework that couples temporal range modeling with latent anchor-layout estimation and deterministic distance projection. GAIA preserves range denoising as the supervised task while orienting the learned distances toward boundary-consistent reconstruction. We evaluate GAIA on a real-world outdoor UWB dataset with synchronized UWB, GNSS, and IMU measurements, and further test robustness using a real-data-calibrated stress-test simulator. GAIA achieves the lowest overall range MSE and highest polygon IoU among evaluated filtering-based and learning-based baselines, reducing MSE by 18.4% and improving polygon IoU by 15.5% over PoseMLP. These results show that geometry-aware range denoising provides an effective path toward spatially coherent work-zone reconstruction.

</details>


### [60] [The Granularity Paradox: How Temporal Disaggregation Inflates In-Sample Fit and Compounds Out-of-Sample Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05450)
*Hugo Moreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时间序列预测中的"粒度悖论"：更细的时间粒度（如从月度到周/日）虽能提升样本内诊断效果并增加数据量，但由于递归误差在更长预测步长上累积，反而降低样本外预测精度；粗粒度（年度）虽消除递归误差传播，但减少可用数据。通过13年公共采购数据集对6种粒度下10种模型的基准测试，发现非线性阈值结构：递归自回归和季节性模型在高频预测中显著退化（如Holt-Winters在日粒度下R²为-151，TPFE达425.85%），而LSTM呈现U形误差曲线（从月度的19.66%恶化至双周的35.94%，后在日粒度克服误差累积惩罚，TPFE降至4.35%，R²为0.66）。线性回归在所有粒度下保持稳定（TPFE 16.3-17.0%），证实悖论源于递归反馈拓扑而非模型复杂度。标准点状指标（RMSE、MAE）系统性地掩盖了累积误差传播，提出了共识-分歧诊断方法，通过比较点状指标与累积TPFE的方向行为来识别被标准诊断掩盖系统误差传播的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中，时间粒度选择（粗粒度 vs. 细粒度）存在根本性矛盾：细粒度增加数据量但导致递归误差累积，粗粒度减少递归误差但限制数据可用性。现有研究未系统形式化这一权衡，且标准评估指标（RMSE、MAE）无法捕捉累积误差传播效应，导致模型评估误导。

Method: 形式化粒度悖论中的误差权衡关系，在6种时间粒度（从年度到日）下对10种模型（包括朴素、统计、机器学习和深度学习架构）进行基准测试，使用13年公共采购数据集，并引入共识-分歧诊断方法比较点状指标与累积TPFE的方向行为。

Result: 发现非线性阈值结构：递归自回归和季节性模型在高频下严重退化（Holt-Winters在日粒度R²=-151，TPFE=425.85%）；LSTM呈U形误差曲线（从月度19.66%到日粒度4.35%）；线性回归稳定（16.3-17.0% TPFE）。标准点状指标系统性地掩盖累积误差传播。

Conclusion: 粒度悖论主要由递归反馈拓扑驱动而非模型复杂度；标准点状指标用于评估预测时会产生误导性结论；需采用目标依赖的累积指标；共识-分歧诊断可有效识别被标准诊断掩盖的系统误差传播模型。

Abstract: This paper explores the "Granularity Paradox" in time-series forecasting, wherein finer temporal disaggregation (e.g., Monthly to Weekly/Daily) improves in-sample diagnostics and dataset size (N), but degrades out-of-sample accuracy due to recursive error compounding over longer horizons (H). Conversely, coarse aggregation (Annual) eliminates recursive error propagation but reduces data available to estimators. We formalize this trade-off and benchmark 10 models - spanning naïve, statistical, machine learning, and deep learning architectures - across six granularities using a 13-year public procurement dataset. The empirical results reveal a non-monotonic threshold structure: recursive autoregressive and seasonal models degrade substantially under high-frequency forecasting (e.g., Holt-Winters reaches a Test R-squared of -151 and TPFE of 425.85% at the Daily grain), while the LSTM traces a U-shaped error curve, worsening from Monthly (19.66%) through Bi-Weekly (35.94%) before overcoming the error propagation penalty at Daily (TPFE of 4.35%, R-squared of 0.66). Linear Regression remains stable across all granularities (16.3-17.0% TPFE), confirming that the paradox is driven by recursive feedback topology, not model complexity. The results demonstrate that standard pointwise metrics (RMSE, MAE) systematically mask cumulative error propagation, and that evaluating forecasts without goal-dependent cumulative metrics produces misleading assessments of model adequacy. We introduce a consensus-dissensus diagnostic comparing the directional behaviour of pointwise metrics against cumulative TPFE across granularities, enabling the identification of models whose standard diagnostics mask systematic error propagation.

</details>


### [61] [Exogenous Dropout: A Simple, Strong Baseline for Corruption-Robust Time Series Forecasting with Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05452)
*Hao Hu,Xue-shan Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出外生丢弃法（exogenous dropout），一种在训练时随机将外生通道归零的模型无关方法，能显著提升时间序列预测模型在外生变量受噪声、时间错位或缺失时的鲁棒性，且无需专门架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在使用外生协变量时，部署中极易因协变量出现噪声、时间错位或缺失而性能严重下降，甚至劣于仅用内生变量的基线模型。研究旨在探索是否无需专门架构设计，仅通过简单的训练干预即可获得这种鲁棒性。

Method: 提出外生丢弃法（exogenous dropout），在训练过程中随机将整个外生通道置零，是一种模型无关的简单训练干预方法。在电力价格预测、水库水文和气象三个领域进行实验，并与显式架构约束方法BoundEx（含可学习门控、回退残差连接和外生FiLM调制）进行对比。

Result: 外生丢弃法在服从高斯噪声、时间错位和通道完全缺失的情况下显著提升了鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据上的精度。应用于双相关网络时，成为实验中最鲁棒的模型，超越BoundEx。架构消融实验表明，显式的架构边界约束并非鲁棒性的必要条件——使用外生丢弃法训练的无界模型在所有领域均比有界模型更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 外生丢弃法是一种简单、强基线方法，推荐作为未来外生协变量时间序列预测研究的标配。显式的架构边界对于外生鲁棒性并非必需，简单的训练干预即可实现更优的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Time series forecasters that use exogenous covariates are fragile in deployment: when those covariates are noised, temporally misaligned, or missing, strong exogenous-fusion and exogenous-adapted models can degrade far above the endogenous-only floor. We study whether such robustness requires specialized architectures, or whether it can be obtained through a simple training intervention. We propose exogenous dropout, a model-agnostic method that randomly zeros whole exogenous channels during training. Across electricity-price forecasting, reservoir hydrology, and meteorology, exogenous dropout substantially improves robustness under Gaussian noise, temporal misalignment, and fully missing channels, while preserving clean accuracy. Applied to a dual-correlation network, it yields the most robust model in our experiments, outperforming a deliberately strong bounded architectural foil, BoundEx, which combines a learnable gate, a fallback residual to the endogenous backbone, and per-channel exogenous FiLM modulation. Architecture-by-dropout ablations, gate-behavior diagnostics, and a representation-level bound show that explicit architectural boundedness is not necessary for this robustness: an unbounded model trained with exogenous dropout is more robust than the bounded model in every domain. We release a corruption-robustness benchmark and recommend exogenous dropout as a simple, strong baseline for future work on time series forecasting with covariates.

</details>


### [62] [Empirical Minimal-Realisation Compression of Deep Neural Networks via Controllability-Observability Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05457)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可控性-可观测性框架的深度神经网络状态阶约简方法，通过构建数据驱动的Gramian矩阵计算各层可达秩、可观测秩和平衡秩，并以此作为压缩后的层宽。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，该方法实现了70-73%的状态压缩和73-83%的参数压缩，准确率几乎无损，推理延迟降低约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络存在大量隐藏状态冗余，现有压缩方法（权重剪枝、量化等）未明确刻画内部状态的动态角色，缺乏从系统理论角度进行状态阶约简的 principled 方法。

Method: 将训练后的深度网络视为深度索引的非线性动力系统，从隐藏状态快照和输出雅可比矩阵构建数据驱动的可达性Gramian、可观测性Gramian和平衡Gramian，通过A/B/C检验估计层级的可达秩、可观测秩及联合可达-可观测秩，并将这些秩作为压缩后的层宽构建精简网络。

Result: MNIST上，4层SiLU DNN从状态阶1024压缩至277（72.95%状态压缩，73.48%参数压缩），精度95.45% vs 全模型96.60%；CIFAR-10上，更大SiLU DNN从4608压缩至1339（70.94%状态压缩，83.09%参数压缩），精度54.44% vs 54.45%，CUDA推理延迟降低约3倍。

Conclusion: 平衡可达-可观测秩为设计紧凑神经网络提供了基于经验最小实现准则的理论依据，能够在几乎不损失精度的前提下实现显著的状态和参数压缩。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often contain substantial hidden-state redundancy, but most compression methods operate directly on weights, neurons, or quantised representations without explicitly characterising the dynamical role of internal states. This paper proposes a controllability-observability framework for empirical state-order reduction of deep neural networks. By viewing a trained network as a depth-indexed nonlinear dynamical system, we construct data-driven reachability, observability, and balanced Gramians from hidden-state snapshots and output Jacobians. The resulting A/B/C tests estimate layer-wise reachable, observable, and jointly reachable--observable ranks. These ranks are then used not only as diagnostic measures of hidden-state redundancy, but also as actual compressed layer widths for realised reduced networks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 compare the proposed balanced realisation against projection-based reduction, unstructured pruning, structured pruning, low-rank SVD, dynamic INT8 quantisation, and linear baselines. On MNIST, a four-layer SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 1024 to 277, giving 72.95% state compression and 73.48% parameter compression, while maintaining 95.45% accuracy compared with 96.60% for the full model. On CIFAR-10, a larger SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 4608 to 1339, giving 70.94% state compression and 83.09% parameter compression, while preserving accuracy from 54.45% to 54.44% and reducing CUDA inference latency by approximately 3X. The results show that balanced reachable-observable ranks provide a principled empirical minimal-realisation criterion for designing compact neural architectures with little or no loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [63] [Learning to Control LLM Agent Harnesses with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05458)
*Haiwen Yi,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM代理的执行框架本身视为可学习的控制层，通过Harness MDP和离线训练让轻量级控制器学习结构化执行动作，在冻结LLM的情况下提升验证行为和任务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的改进集中于提示词、模型或手工工作流，而执行框架被视为固定基础设施。作者认为框架本身是可学习的控制层，应进行优化。

Method: 形式化框架操作为有限时域Harness MDP，轻量级控制器选择结构化执行动作，冻结LLM执行器。使用优势加权回归从离线轨迹训练，仅用终端任务评分奖励。引入Harness成熟度评分分离最终任务质量与过程行为。

Result: 在6个受控域和2个公共基准适配器上，学习到的控制器一致提升验证行为，选择性提升最终任务质量。在tau-bench零售、AgentBench DB-Bench和编码任务上增益最大。消融实验表明增益无法通过行为克隆或简单增加检查来解释。

Conclusion: 框架控制是可学习的层，能提升冻结LLM代理的性能，但离线数据支撑限制了更好的过程控制何时能转化为更好的最终答案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are usually improved by changing prompts, models, or hand-written workflows, while the execution harness around the model is treated as fixed infrastructure. We argue that this harness is itself a learnable control layer. We formalize harness operation as a finite-horizon Harness MDP, where a lightweight controller selects structural execution actions while the LLM executor remains frozen. The controller is trained from offline rollouts using advantage-weighted regression with only terminal task-rubric rewards. We also separate final task quality from a post-hoc Harness Maturity Score, which measures whether the harness follows reliable execution patterns rather than only whether the final answer is correct. This separation gives a finite-buffer view of harness learning: final-quality gains require high-return support in the offline buffer, while process behavior can shift whenever it aligns with advantage-weighted actions. Across six controlled domains and two public-benchmark adapters, the learned controller consistently improves verification behavior and selectively improves final task quality, with the largest gains on adapted tau-bench retail, adapted AgentBench DB-Bench, and coding with a calibrated structural verifier. Ablations against behavior cloning and Forced CHECK show that the gains are not explained by imitation or by simply adding checks. These results identify harness control as a learnable layer for frozen LLM agents, while showing that offline support limits when better process control becomes better final answers.

</details>


### [64] [AdaStop: Cost-Aware Early Stopping for DNN Test Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05461)
*Bonan Shen,Wei-Jung Huang,Xin Liu,Jiazhou Gao,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaStop框架，将DNN测试建模为成本-收益决策过程，通过估计边际故障发现率并在其低于阈值c/v时停止标注，从而在不预先设定标注预算的情况下平衡测试成本与收益。实验表明仅用9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN测试方法需要在固定标注预算下选择最可能揭示故障的输入，但实际中很难选择合适的预算：预算过少会漏检故障，预算过多则造成不必要的标注成本。因此需要一种能自动决定何时停止测试的机制。

Method: 将DNN测试形式化为成本-收益决策过程：标注一个输入产生成本c，发现一个故障产生价值v；提出AdaStop框架，在测试过程中持续估计边际故障发现率，当估计率低于阈值τ=c/v时停止标注。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和选择策略上的实验表明，仅使用9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障。

Conclusion: AdaStop通过动态停止机制有效平衡了DNN测试中的成本与收益，避免了固定预算带来的过少或过多标注问题，大幅提高了标注效率。

Abstract: Existing methods for testing deep neural networks (DNNs) primarily prioritize test inputs likely to reveal model faults under a fixed labeling budget. In practice, choosing that budget is difficult: too little testing misses failures, while too much incurs unnecessary labeling costs. This work studies the stopping problem in DNN testing. We formulate testing as a cost--benefit decision process in which labeling an input incurs cost $c$ and discovering a fault yields value $v$. Based on this formulation, we introduce \textit{AdaStop}, a framework that estimates the marginal fault discovery rate during testing and stops labeling when the estimated rate falls below the threshold $τ= c/v$. Experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and selection strategies show that $65$--$84\%$ of faults can be discovered using only $9$--$31\%$ of the labeling budget.

</details>


### [65] [Learnable Weighting of Intra-Attribute Distances for Categorical Data Clustering with Nominal and Ordinal Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05464)
*Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图视角的统一距离度量方法，同时处理名义型和序数型属性，并设计将距离权重学习与数据划分融合为单一范式的聚类算法，避免次优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类数据聚类方法未区分名义型与序数型属性的本质差异，忽略了序数值的相对顺序信息以及两类属性间的相互依赖关系，导致距离度量不够准确。

Method: 从图视角统一建模名义型和序数型属性的类内距离，在保留序数值顺序关系的同时实现统一度量；提出新型聚类算法，将属性距离权重学习与数据对象划分整合为单一学习范式，而非两步分离流程。

Result: 实验结果表明所提算法在聚类性能上优于现有对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过图视角统一处理名义型和序数型属性距离，并将权重学习与聚类融合，有效提升了分类数据聚类的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The success of categorical data clustering generally much relies on the distance metric that measures the dissimilarity degree between two objects. However, most of the existing clustering methods treat the two categorical subtypes, i.e. nominal and ordinal attributes, in the same way when calculating the dissimilarity without considering the relative order information of the ordinal values. Moreover, there would exist interdependence among the nominal and ordinal attributes, which is worth exploring for indicating the dissimilarity. This paper will therefore study the intrinsic difference and connection of nominal and ordinal attribute values from a perspective akin to the graph. Accordingly, we propose a novel distance metric to measure the intra-attribute distances of nominal and ordinal attributes in a unified way, meanwhile preserving the order relationship among ordinal values. Subsequently, we propose a new clustering algorithm to make the learning of intra-attribute distance weights and partitions of data objects into a single learning paradigm rather than two separate steps, whereby circumventing a suboptimal solution. Experiments show the efficacy of the proposed algorithm in comparison with the existing counterparts.

</details>


### [66] [Breaking Structural Isolation: Scalable Graph Clustering via Community-Aware Sampling and Structural Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05469)
*Jingyun Zhang,Hao Peng,Jianxin Li,Angsheng Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCISE是一个可扩展的无监督图聚类框架，通过社区感知采样与约束结构熵协同作用，在保持拓扑完整性的同时解决小批量训练中的"结构隔离"问题，在六个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图对比学习的无监督聚类方法在迷你批次训练中普遍存在"结构隔离"问题，难以捕捉表征全局拓扑分布的凝聚性社区结构，导致社区碎片化和全局信息损失。

Method: 提出了三个核心模块：(1) 结构熵社区约束算子(SECC)，在约束解空间中优化结构信息以减少社区碎片化；(2) 社区感知采样扩展机制(CSampE)，将目标节点的社区上下文融入采样批次以保持拓扑完整性；(3) 结构对比学习模块(StructCL)，基于批次内结构相似性细化边权重，引导编码器在高阶结构空间中学习表示。

Result: 在六个主流基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SCISE显著优于最先进算法，消融研究和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其在真实大规模图上的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: SCISE通过社区感知采样与结构熵约束的协同设计，有效缓解了图聚类中的结构隔离问题，为大规模图的无监督聚类提供了一种可扩展且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised graph clustering is a fundamental technique for uncovering underlying semantic patterns in large-scale networks. Although Graph Contrastive Learning has demonstrated promising performance, existing methods often suffer from the "structural isolation" issue during mini-batch training, making it challenging to capture cohesive community structures that characterize the global topological distribution. To address these challenges, we propose SCISE, a Scalable unsupervised graph Clustering framework that preserves structural Integrity by synergizing community-aware sampling with constrained Structural Entropy. Specifically, we first introduce the Structural Entropy Community Constraint operator (SECC), which optimizes structural information within a constrained solution space to mitigate community fragmentation and enhance partition cohesion. Second, to prevent global information loss during batch training, we design a Community-Aware Sampling Expansion (CSampE) mechanism that incorporates the community context of target nodes into sampling batches, effectively breaking structural barriers and preserving topological integrity. Finally, we devise a Structural Contrastive Learning (StructCL) module that refines edge weights based on intra-batch structural similarity, guiding the encoder to learn representations in a higher-order structural space. Extensive experiments on six mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCISE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms, with ablation studies and robustness analyses further validating its effectiveness and reliability for real-world large-scale graphs.

</details>


### [67] [Parameter-Free Encoders Remain Viable for RDB Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05476)
*Linjie Xu,David Wipf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在关系数据库（RDB）中预测缺失值时，是否真的需要参数化编码器进行预训练。作者通过理论分析和实验证明，简单的无参数子图编码器结合单表基础模型，在标签信息作为输入时，能够达到与复杂参数化编码器相当的性能，挑战了当前主流观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库中预测任务众多，为每个新任务从头训练模型成本高昂。当前学界对RDB基础模型的设计存在分歧：一方认为无参数编码器结合单表基础模型即可达到接近最优性能，无需预训练；另一方则主张参数化编码器需要通过预训练学习任务特定表示。作者旨在解决这一争议，明确标签作为输入时编码器的设计原则。

Method: 作者首先从理论上分析RDB编码器在标签作为输入时的性质，证明了可训练编码器参数在潜在效能上存在局限性。随后通过大量基准任务进行实验验证，将简单的无参数编码器与复杂的参数化编码器进行性能对比。

Result: 理论分析证明了可训练编码器参数在标签作为输入时存在效能上限；实验结果表明，简单的无参数编码器在多个相关基准任务中依然能够表现出强劲的性能，与复杂参数化方法可比。

Conclusion: 在RDB预测任务中，当标签信息已作为输入可用时，复杂的可训练编码器参数并非必要，简单的无参数编码器足以实现强性能。这为RDB基础模型的设计提供了简化方向。

Abstract: Given a relational database (RDB) storing heterogeneous tabular information, how can we predict missing (or future) values in some target column of interest? As the space of potential targets is vast across enterprise settings, it is preferable to avoid learning a new model from scratch each time there is a new prediction task. Frozen foundation models based on RDB-specific encoders provide a viable solution, but ideal design remains an open question. On the one hand, it has recently been argued that certain parameter-free subgraph encoders combined with single-table foundation models can achieve near SOTA performance, with no RDB-specific pre-training required. Meanwhile, other contemporary studies advocate for parameterized encoders pre-trained to exploit observable labels for learning task-specific representations. To address this ambiguity, we analyze RDB encoder properties specifically when labels are present as inputs, proving limitations on the potential efficacy of trainable encoder parameters. As empirical validation, we demonstrate that considerably simpler parameter-free encoders are still capable of strong performance across many relevant benchmarking tasks.

</details>


### [68] [InvWeaver: Deductive Feedback for Invariant Synthesis in Interacting-Loop Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05478)
*Guangyuan Wu,Weining Cao,Zehui Tan,Yuan Yao,Hengfeng Wei,Taolue Chen,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InvWeaver是一个神经符号框架，通过循环级抽象、义务引导推理和最弱前置条件精炼来暴露循环间依赖关系并传播证明义务，从而为多循环程序合成不变量。在82个多循环基准问题中解决了72个，并保持了单循环任务上的强劲性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型辅助的猜测与验证技术在处理单循环程序时表现良好，但难以处理包含多个交互循环的程序，因此需要一种能够有效处理循环间依赖关系的不变量推断方法。

Method: 提出了InvWeaver神经符号框架，核心策略包括：(1) 循环级抽象（loop-level abstraction）暴露循环间依赖；(2) 义务引导推理（obligation-guided inference）传播证明义务；(3) 最弱前置条件基的精炼（weakest-precondition-based refinement）进行验证与迭代优化。

Result: 在82个多循环基准问题中成功解决72个，显著优于现有不变量推断方法，同时在单循环任务上也保持了强劲性能。

Conclusion: InvWeaver通过神经符号方法有效解决了多循环程序的不变量推断难题，为程序验证领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Loop invariant inference is a fundamental yet challenging problem in program verification. Recent LLM-aided guess-and-check techniques have shown strong performance on single-loop programs, but they often struggle with programs containing multiple interacting loops. This paper presents InvWeaver, a neuro-symbolic framework for synthesizing invariants for such programs. The key idea is to expose inter-loop dependencies and propagate proof obligations through a combination of loop-level abstraction, obligation-guided inference, and weakest-precondition-based refinement. We evaluate InvWeaver on a comprehensive benchmark suite, including a newly curated dataset derived from classic algorithms. Experimental results show that InvWeaver substantially outperforms existing invariant inference methods, solving 72 out of 82 multi-loop benchmark problems and maintaining strong performance on single-loop tasks.

</details>


### [69] [PatchOptic for Shared-State LLM Workflows with Projected Views and Verified Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05483)
*Zhaoyu Bai,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PatchOptic是一种基于光学（optic）启发的接口，用于共享状态下的LLM工作流。它通过投影读取和验证的结构化补丁，在工作流步骤中声明读视图、授权写区域和补丁源区域，实现局部更新与全局有效性的契约。实验表明该方法能减少信息泄露和token消耗，同时保持输出质量，并能在提交前阻断违反工作流契约的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的渐进式披露方法（如关键词搜索、RAG、AST查询等）虽然解决了读取端的问题，但未定义局部提出的改写在写回完整状态后是否仍然有效，缺少局部更新与全局有效性之间的契约。

Method: 提出PatchOptic接口，借鉴光学（optic）的视图/更新思想，通过投影读取和验证的结构化补丁实现。每个工作流步骤声明投影读视图、授权写区域和补丁源区域；支持运行时强制、路径级足迹、委托、子工作流组合，以及同一阶段内独立步骤重排序的静态证书。

Result: 在包含46个案例的PatchBench基准测试上评估，投影读取减少了信息泄露和token消耗，同时保持了强actor下的可接受输出质量。运行时验证能在提交前阻断声明的工作流契约违反，补丁读取强制能拒绝使用隐藏源的受损补丁工件。

Conclusion: PatchOptic通过形式化的视图/更新契约有效解决了LLM工作流中局部更新与全局一致性的问题，兼顾了安全性（减少泄露、阻断违规）和效率（减少token消耗）。

Abstract: Agentic workflows often operate over shared, structured state. Because LLM context windows are limited, each model invocation is typically shown only the state fragment needed for the current workflow step, a pattern commonly known as progressive disclosure.
