We formalize this property as \textbf{endpoint decodability} and show that the decoder is the minimum-MSE estimator $\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$ under the usual $\ell_2$ objective. This yields \textbf{Truncated Jump Sampling} (TJS): stop the ODE at an early-exit time $t^*$ and return the decoded $x_0$. TJS requires no retraining, distillation, or architecture change. Across SDXL, SD3.5M, Z-Image-Turbo, and three class-conditional benchmarks, it reduces NFEs by 20--70\% with near-matched quality. The analysis also shows why endpoint prediction can work without straightening the trajectory, providing inference acceleration without trajectory redesign.

</details>


### [105] [Self-Supervised Implicit CEST Reconstruction via Physics-Informed Lorentz Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06132)
*Dexuan Li,Yupeng Wu,Chenglong Wang,Hanlin Liu,Hui Zhen,Jianqi Li,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理信息的洛伦兹编码（LE）框架，用于化学交换饱和转移（CEST）MRI的稀疏采样重建，通过隐式连续坐标学习将物理约束融入重建过程，显著提升重建质量和代谢物定量映射准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEST MRI受限于长采集时间，稀疏采样可缩短时间但重建高分辨率Z谱是病态逆问题。现有插值和隐式神经表示方法缺乏物理约束，导致频谱伪影和物理无效信号。

Method: 提出Lorentz Encoding（LE）物理信息框架，将CEST重建公式化为自监督重建任务，通过隐式连续坐标学习实现。不同于通用位置编码，LE通过可学习基函数的参数化洛伦兹分布组合，将稀疏坐标投影到物理约束空间进行正则化。

Result: 在39点采样下，LE方法PSNR达57.58 dB，SSIM达0.9994，显著优于现有方法。学习到的物理信息编码在潜空间中形成连续、几何有序的轨迹，确保APT、NOE、MT等代谢物定量映射的准确性。

Conclusion: LE框架有效解决了CEST稀疏采样重建中的病态问题，通过物理约束消除了频谱伪影，实现了高精度代谢物定量成像。

Abstract: Multi-Pool Chemical Exchange Saturation Transfer (CEST) MRI provides valuable metabolic information but is clinically limited by long acquisition times. Although sparse sampling reduces scanning time, reconstructing high-resolution Z-spectra from limited data remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Conventional interpolation and generic Implicit Neural Rep-resentations (INRs) often lack physical constraints, leading to spectral artifacts and physically invalid signals. To address this, we propose Lorentz Encoding (LE), a physics-informed framework that formulates CEST reconstruction as a self-supervised reconstruction task via implicit continuous coordinate learning. Unlike generic positional encodings, LE regularizes the continuous spectral mapping by projecting sparse coordinates into a physically constrained space governed by a combination of parametric Lorentzian profiles with learnable basis functions. This mechanism effectively reduces noise and enforces consistency with physical models. Experiments on in vivo human brain data demonstrate that LE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Specifically, under a 39-point sampling strategy, LE achieves a PSNR of 57.58 dB and an SSIM of 0.9994. Furthermore, the learned physics-informed encodings form a continuous, geometrically ordered trajectory in the latent space, ensuring accurate quantitative metabo-lite mapping (APT, NOE, MT).

</details>


### [106] [Leveraging Extragradient for Effective Sharpness-Aware Minimization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06151)
*Yao Fu,Chunxia Zhang,Junmin Liu,Yihang Jin,Haishan Ye,Yuanao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EISAM优化器，通过外梯度技术改进SAM，以两步更新过程（预测步+扰动步）实现更好的泛化性能，降低对扰动半径的敏感度，在多个基准数据集上优于SGD、Adam和SAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的泛化挑战：传统优化器（如SGD）易收敛到尖锐最小值，导致过拟合；已有SAM方法虽能寻找平坦最小值，但仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出EISAM（Extragradient-Inspired Sharpness-Aware Minimization），采用两步更新：预测步（探测损失景观几何结构）和扰动步（用基础优化器精炼更新），通过外梯度技术增强泛化。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，EISAM在测试准确率和训练效率方面一致优于SGD、Adam和SAM；降低了对扰动半径的敏感性，提高了鲁棒性；理论分析证明其通过将参数导向曲率更小的平坦最小值，收紧泛化界。

Conclusion: EISAM是一种鲁棒、可扩展且广泛适用的优化方案，在理论和实践上推进了深度学习优化，并提供了实用的超参数调优指导。

Abstract: Generalization remains a pivotal challenge in deep learning, where traditional optimizers like Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) often converge to sharp minima, leading to overfitting and reduced performance on unseen data. Building on Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), for seeking flat minima associated with improved generalization, we propose the Extragradient-Inspired Sharpness-Aware Minimization (EISAM), a novel optimizer that enhances generalization via the extragradient technique. EISAM uses a two-step update process: a prediction step investigating the geometry of the loss landscape and a perturbation step that refines updates with a base optimizer. This approach achieves better generalization performance than SAM. Crucially, EISAM reduces sensitivity to the perturbation radius, enhancing robustness, and simplifying the tuning across diverse settings. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that EISAM consistently outperforms SGD, Adaptive Moment Estimation (Adam), and SAM in test accuracy and training efficiency across various architectures. Theoretical analysis further confirms that EISAM tightens the generalization bound by steering parameters toward flatter minima with reduced curvature. Accompanied by a thorough hyperparameter analysis, EISAM offers practical tuning guidance, establishing it as a robust, scalable, and broadly applicable optimization solution that advances both the theory and practice in deep learning.

</details>


### [107] [KVpop -- Key-Value Cache Compression with Predictive Online Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05061)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Thomas Schmied,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVpop是一种通过未来注意力信号监督的KV缓存驱逐策略，在75%压缩率下保持98%的全注意力性能，88%压缩率下保持97%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存增长是自回归解码的主要瓶颈，现有驱逐方法依赖静态启发式或代理分数，难以追踪未来token效用，导致脆弱的驱逐决策。

Method: 提出KVpop，通过学习固定预算的KV驱逐策略，直接监督保留或丢弃决策。训练使用新颖的未来注意力目标，无需实例化密集注意力图高效计算。引入延迟记忆评分器，推迟固定步数后评分以利用近期上下文。

Result: 在AIME和HMMT数学推理任务上，Qwen3-4B在75%压缩率下保留98%性能，88%压缩率下保留97%性能，持续优于现有驱逐基线。Qwen3-8B达到接近教师模型的完整性能。

Conclusion: 使用未来注意力信号监督驱逐可以在降低内存成本的同时保持生成质量，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache growth is a major bottleneck in autoregressive decoding, as memory and bandwidth scale linearly with context length. Existing KV eviction methods often rely on static heuristics or proxy scores, which poorly track future token utility and cause brittle eviction as relevance shifts. To address this, we introduce KVpop, which learns a fixed-budget KV eviction policy by directly supervising the keep-or-drop decision. The scorer is trained against a novel future-attention target, computed efficiently without materializing dense attention maps. We further introduce a delayed memory-based scorer that, uniquely among learned eviction methods, defers scoring for a fixed number of steps to exploit near-future context. On AIME and HMMT mathematical reasoning, KVpop retains 98% of full-attention performance on Qwen3-4B at 75% KV cache compression and 97% at 88% compression, consistently outperforming established eviction baselines. Qwen3-8B shows even stronger results, reaching near-full teacher performance. These results show that supervising eviction with future-attention signals cuts memory costs while maintaining quality.

</details>


### [108] [X-FEMR: A Token-level Explainable Approach for Electronic Health Records Foundation Models using Transformer-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06163)
*Jie Huang,Pengfei Yin,Zihan Xu,Daniel Capurro,Mike Conway,Ting Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对电子健康记录的基础模型（FEMRs）作为黑盒模型存在可解释性不足的问题，本文提出了首个token级可解释性方法：通过训练基于Transformer的代理模型来近似FEMR行为，识别关键token，并引入临床对齐度量评估解释的临床相关性。实验表明代理模型能很好地逼近FEMR预测，且token级解释与临床知识高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FEMRs虽然在临床预测任务中表现优异，但作为黑盒模型缺乏可解释性，引发了关于偏见、可解释性和临床信任的担忧。

Method: 提出首个token级可解释性方法：在两个预测任务上，训练基于Transformer的代理模型，利用FEMR的输入-输出对近似其行为并保留时间动态；通过识别最具影响力的token来揭示FEMR如何利用患者历史信息；引入新的临床对齐度量来量化代理模型关键token与临床验证特征之间的一致性。

Result: 代理模型能够紧密逼近FEMR的预测结果；token级解释与临床知识具有良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为可解释且可信赖的临床AI提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Foundation Models for Electronic Health Records (FEMRs) are pretrained on large-scale structured patient data, enabling them to convert longitudinal patient trajectories into generalizable representations for diverse clinical prediction tasks. Despite their effectiveness, FEMRs remain black-box models, raising concerns about bias, interpretability, and clinical trust. To address this, we propose the first token-level explainability approach for FEMRs. We train a Transformer-based surrogate model on input-output pairs from the FEMR across two prediction tasks, approximating its behavior while preserving temporal dynamics. We identify the most influential tokens, providing insights into how FEMRs leverage different aspects of patient history for predictions. To evaluate clinical relevance, we introduce a novel clinical alignment metric that quantifies the correspondence between the surrogate model's key tokens and clinically validated features. Our results demonstrate that the surrogate closely approximates FEMR predictions and that token-level explanations align well with clinical knowledge, offering a practical framework for interpretable and trustworthy clinical AI.

</details>


### [109] [Canopy: A Heterograph Foundation Model for Metabolic Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06224)
*Jake Bowden,Laurence Legon,Satnam Surae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Canopy是一个异构图基础模型，整合了10个公开和专有数据源构建包含690万节点、13种节点类型和34种边类型的知识图谱，通过领域特定基础模型编码节点特征，采用异构图Transformer进行预训练，在发酵滴度预测任务上R²=0.41，显著优于表格基线（R²=0.24）和同构图GNN变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代谢工程计算方法要么依赖基于化学计量的约束模型无法从实验数据中学习，要么应用表格机器学习处理手工特征而丢弃了生物知识的 relational 结构，因此需要一种能够整合多源异构数据并利用图结构进行学习的方法。

Method: 构建包含690万节点、13种类型和34种边类型的异构图知识图谱，整合10个数据源；使用ESM-2、MoLFormer和PubMedBERT分别编码蛋白质、化学分子和文本节点；采用HGT（异构图Transformer）架构，结合SignNet位置编码、Jumping Knowledge聚合和虚拟节点，通过四种自监督目标（链接预测、掩码节点建模、距离预测、对比实验聚类）进行预训练，并使用同方差不确定性加权平衡损失。

Result: 在发酵滴度预测下游任务中，冻结的Canopy嵌入搭配轻量级探测头取得R²=0.41，优于表格基线（最佳R²=0.24）和同构图GNN变体。

Conclusion: Canopy展示了异构图表征学习在代谢工程中整合多源生物知识并提升预测性能的潜力，证明了基于知识图谱的预训练方法能够有效捕捉生物系统的关系结构，优于传统表格化方法。

Abstract: Designing microbial strains that produce high-value chemicals at commercially viable titers remains a central challenge in metabolic engineering. Existing computational approaches either rely on stoichiometric constraint-based models that cannot learn from experimental data, or apply tabular machine learning to hand-crafted features that discard the relational structure of biological knowledge. We present Canopy, a heterogeneous graph foundation model that integrates ten public and proprietary data sources into a unified knowledge graph (KG) of 6.9M nodes across 13 types and 34 edge types, covering genes, proteins, metabolites, reactions, pathways, strains, and fermentation experiments. Node features are encoded through domain-specific foundation models (ESM-2 for protein sequences, MoLFormer for chemical SMILES, and PubMedBERT for biomedical text), yielding a multi-modal representation within a single graph. We pretrain a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer (HGT) augmented with SignNet positional encodings, Jumping Knowledge aggregation, and virtual nodes using four self-supervised objectives (link prediction, masked node modelling, distance prediction, and contrastive experiment clustering), balanced via learned homoscedastic uncertainty weighting. On the downstream task of fermentation titer prediction, frozen Canopy embeddings achieve $R^{2} = 0.41$ with a lightweight probe, outperforming tabular baselines (best $R^{2} = 0.24$) and homogeneous GNN variants.

</details>


### [110] [Quantitative Gaussian-Process limits of Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06290)
*Andrea Agazzi,Eloy Mosig García,Dario Trevisan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过张量程序框架研究随机神经网络的无限宽高斯过程极限，给出了Wasserstein距离下的定量收敛理论，主要结果为有限宽度误差界提供了显式表达式，其收敛速度为宽度平方根倒数量级，且框架与架构无关，涵盖前馈模型以及循环和Transformer架构中的权重共享方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对神经网络无限宽极限的理论分析缺乏定量收敛速度的刻画，特别是不同架构（如前馈、循环、Transformer）下的统一收敛理论尚不完善。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供架构无关的显式误差界。

Method: 利用张量程序（tensor programs）框架对随机神经网络进行分析，在Wasserstein距离下建立有限宽度网络与高斯过程极限之间的定量收敛关系，推导出显式的宽度平方根倒数阶误差界。

Result: 得到了有限宽度网络执行结果与其高斯过程极限之间的误差上界，该误差以宽度的平方根倒数量级收敛，且该结果适用于前馈网络、循环网络和Transformer等多种架构。

Conclusion: 本文为随机神经网络的无限宽高斯过程极限提供了首个架构无关的定量收敛理论，统一了多种架构类型的收敛分析，为理解神经网络在宽度趋于无穷时的行为提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study the infinite-width Gaussian-process limit of random neural networks
  through the lens of tensor programs, and we provide a quantitative convergence
  theory in Wasserstein distance.
  Our main result gives explicit finite-width error bounds, of order inverse square-root of the widths
  between finite-network executions and their
  Gaussian-process limits. The framework is architecture-agnostic and covers feed-forward models together
  with weight-sharing schemes relevant for recurrent and transformer-type
  architectures.

</details>


### [111] [Physics-Informed Neural Embeddings of PDE Solution Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06348)
*Raul Jimenez,Svitlana Mayboroda,Pavlos Protopapas,Leonid Sarieddine,David N. Spergel,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，用于学习偏微分方程解族的有限维嵌入。该框架使用多头物理信息神经网络，共享体学习解空间的潜在流形，线性头重建不同初始条件下的解。头正交化惩罚消除了潜在表示的退化，并稳定了主成分谱。实验表明，对于 Burgers 方程，仅需 2-4 个主成分即可捕获约 95% 的潜在空间方差。还通过傅里叶壳分析了频率谱，建立了可复现的流形几何观测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏微分方程解空间降维方法缺乏物理信息约束，且潜在表示的退化导致主成分谱不稳定、难以复现。本文旨在构建一个既能嵌入解族、又能提供可复现的流形几何观测指标的物理信息框架。

Method: 采用多头物理信息神经网络（multihead PINN），共享体学习潜在流形，线性头对应不同初始条件重建解；引入头正交化惩罚消除潜在表示退化；通过傅里叶壳分解分析主成分的频率贡献。

Result: 对于 Burgers 方程，潜在维度 nb=20 时，2-4 个主成分捕获约 95% 方差，4-7 个捕获约 99% 方差；热方程和波动方程也有类似压缩效果。傅里叶壳频率分布在训练间具有不变性。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效学习解族的低维流形嵌入，且通过学习得到的谱和主成分可作为解流形几何的稳健可复现观测指标。

Abstract: We introduce a physics-informed framework for learning finite-dimensional embeddings of solution families of partial differential equations. The method uses a multihead Physics-Informed Neural Network in which a shared body learns a latent manifold representing the solution space, while linear heads reconstruct individual solutions associated with different initial conditions. A head-orthogonalization penalty removes degeneracies in the latent representation and stabilizes the principal-component spectrum across training realizations. Because the initial condition is built into the network output by construction, these principal components measure the additional variability the network learns on top of the initial profile, not the full solution itself. We apply the method to the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation, with the heat and wave equations as robustness checks. For a latent dimension $n_b=20$, the learned manifolds exhibit pronounced effective dimensional reduction: for Burgers dynamics, only $2$-$4$ principal components capture about $95\%$ of the latent-space variance, while $4$-$7$ capture about $99\%$, depending on the initial-condition family; the same qualitative compression holds for the heat and wave equations. We also split the wavenumber axis into bands (``Fourier shells'') and measure how much each band contributes to every principal component. The resulting frequency profile is invariant under the change-of-basis freedom that the orthogonalization penalty leaves in the latent space, and is therefore reproducible across independent training runs. More broadly, this establishes the learned spectral profiles and principal components as robust observables of solution-manifold geometry.

</details>


### [112] [TILDE: TILt-based Distributional Erasure for Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06432)
*Naveen George,Naoki Murata,Yuhta Takida,Konda Reddy Mopuri,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TILDE方法，将概念遗忘形式化为分布对齐问题，通过倾斜分布和残差∇-GFlowNet训练实现文本到图像扩散模型中的概念遗忘，在有效遗忘的同时保持生成质量和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念遗忘方法虽然能有效移除目标概念，但往往忽略了保持良性生成的质量、多样性和语义覆盖这一同样关键的性质；现有擦除目标没有明确指定后遗忘分布应如何逼近从零训练的保留模型，导致保留能力仅是更新规则的附带结果。

Method: 提出TILDE（TILt-based Distributional Erasure），将概念遗忘形式化为分布对齐问题：目标是在遗忘约束下，从预训练模型中寻找最小偏离的条件分布，即能量倾斜的无锚点目标，该目标抑制表达概念的图像同时保留每个提示的良性相对质量。通过残差∇-GFlowNet训练学习由遗忘能量引起的分数修正。

Result: 在物体、艺术风格和角色等多个概念上，TILDE在实现强遗忘效果的同时，在保留能力和分布保真度上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TILDE通过分布对齐视角为概念遗忘提供了理论清晰且实践有效的框架，在遗忘与保留之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Concept unlearning in text-to-image diffusion models is critical for safe and practical deployment: with rising privacy concerns, copyright disputes, trademark constraints, and safety regulations, deployed systems must be able to suppress unwanted concepts after training. Existing methods often remove the target concept effectively, but practical unlearning also requires an equally fundamental property: the unlearned model should retain quality, diversity, and semantic coverage on benign generation. The gold standard is a retain-only model trained from scratch without the unwanted data. However, common erasure objectives do not specify which post-unlearning distribution should approximate this reference, leaving retention as an implicit consequence of the update rule. We propose TILDE, TILt-based Distributional Erasure, which formulates concept unlearning as a distributional alignment problem: the desired target is the minimum-deviation conditional distribution from the pretrained model under a forgetting constraint. This energy-tilted, anchor-free target suppresses concept-expressing images while preserving benign relative mass for each prompt. We instantiate this principle with residual $\nabla$-GFlowNet training, which learns the score correction induced by the forget energy relative to the pretrained diffusion model. Across objects, artistic styles, and characters, TILDE achieves strong forgetting while improving retention and distributional fidelity over prior baselines.

</details>


### [113] [EntroPath: Maximum Entropy Path Ensemble Embedding for Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06497)
*Przemysław Rola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntroPath是一种基于最大熵随机游走（MERW）路径集成的流形学习方法，通过自由能差异度量逼近测地线距离，在图嵌入中同时克服了局部归一化随机游走的密度偏差和最短路径对伪边的敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图嵌入方法存在两类问题：局部归一化随机游走易在密集采样区集中扩散，而最短路径距离对图中虚假捷径边敏感。需要一种既能忠实保持底层测地线几何、又对非均匀采样和分支结构鲁棒的流形学习方法。

Method: EntroPath基于最大熵随机游走（MERW）构建点对间的自由能差异度量，利用k步路径的完整集成而非单一轨迹；通过Varadhan热核公式证明其在短时极限下收敛到平方测地线距离；扩散深度k可平滑插值局部邻域结构与全局流形几何；对称化核允许精确的Gram分解以连接核方法；通过地标投影和扩散势伪时间实现可扩展扩展。

Result: 在合成流形和单细胞基准测试中，EntroPath一致达到或优于扩散方法和最短路径方法；在与UMAP、t-SNE等邻域保持嵌入方法的局部结构指标比较中具有竞争力；在非均匀采样密度和分支轨迹分离良好的流形上优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 路径集成扩散相比传统单一轨迹方法能更忠实地保留底层测地线几何，特别适用于密度非均匀和具有分支结构的流形数据。

Abstract: We introduce EntroPath, a manifold learning method that recovers geodesic geometry from data graphs through ensembles of diffusion paths. Many existing graph-based embeddings rely either on locally normalised random walks or on shortest-path distances. The former can concentrate diffusion in densely sampled regions, while the latter are sensitive to spurious shortcut edges in the graph. EntroPath instead builds its dissimilarities from the maximum entropy random walk (MERW), which aggregates the full ensemble of k-step paths between points rather than relying on any single trajectory. We show that the resulting free-energy dissimilarity converges to squared geodesic distance in the short-time limit, via Varadhan's heat-kernel formula. The diffusion depth k interpolates smoothly between local neighbourhood structure and global manifold geometry, and the symmetrised kernel admits an exact Gram factorisation connecting EntroPath to kernel methods. We further provide scalable extensions via landmark projection and diffusion-potential pseudotime. Across synthetic manifolds and single-cell benchmarks, EntroPath consistently matches or outperforms diffusion- and shortest-path-based methods, while remaining competitive with neighbourhood-preserving embeddings (UMAP, t-SNE) on local-structure metrics. Its gains are most pronounced on manifolds with non-uniform sampling density and well-separated branching trajectories, where path-ensemble diffusion more faithfully preserves the underlying geodesic geometry.

</details>


### [114] [GraphBU: MILP Instance Generation with Graph-Native Block Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06532)
*Xiaolei Guo,Chenyu Zhou,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphBU，一种以局部子问题加接口为基本单元的图原生MILP生成器，通过提升耦合节点和兼容性替换生成实例，保持图统计相似度约0.934、可行性约96.7%，并使下游PS训练指标提升约8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MILP生成器无法显式记录局部子问题与实例其余部分的耦合关系，导致生成实例结构失真，难以用于求解器开发和策略学习。

Method: 以局部子问题加接口为基本单元，将耦合节点提升为主约束或边界变量形成块单元，再进行兼容性检查替换；同时保持图结构对行列置换的不变性。

Result: 图统计相似度约0.934，可行性约96.7%，下游Predict-and-Search训练指标提升约8.0%。

Conclusion: GraphBU通过显式建模局部-全局耦合关系，能够生成结构保持良好、可行性高的MILP实例，并有效提升下游学习方法性能。

Abstract: Mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) instances used for solver development are hard to obtain when models come from private or application-specific pipelines. A generator must keep the structure that solvers and learned policies rely on. Existing general generators usually choose their generation unit from a formulation template, summary statistics, local graph edits, or blocks found after recombination. These units do not explicitly record how a local part of the MILP is coupled to the rest of the instance. We propose GraphBU, a graph-native generator whose basic unit is a local subproblem plus its interface. The method promotes coupling nodes into master constraints or boundary variables and uses the resulting block units for compatibility-checked replacement. The analysis focuses on the properties needed by this construction: promotion separates interfaces, replacement can preserve feasibility under an interface-slack condition, and the graph construction is invariant to row-column permutations. On MILP instances generation, this unit keeps graph statistics close to the source family, preserves feasibility on most datasets, and improves downstream Predict-and-Search training. Genrated by GraphBU, The average graph-statistical similarity was approximately 0.934, the average feasibility was approximately 96.7%, and the average increase in the main index of downstream PS was approximately 8.0%.

</details>


### [115] [Graph Convolutional Attention: A Spectral Perspective on Graph Denoising and Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06546)
*Shervin Khalafi,Igor Krawczuk,Sergio Rozada,Charilaos Kanatsoulis,Antonio G Marques,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图卷积注意力（GCA）机制，理论证明线性注意力在图去噪任务中仅能学习平均谱滤波器，存在根本性局限；GCA通过利用输入图谱实现谱自适应去噪，并在Stochastic Block Models上达到理论最优。实验表明GCA可提升图去噪与扩散性能，在DiGress中替代标准图Transformer无需昂贵结构特征，结合PEARL位置编码可避免显式特征分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的图去噪方法（尤其是线性注意力）缺乏理论理解，不清楚标准注意力是否是图去噪的正确机制。线性注意力仅能学习训练分布上的平均谱滤波器，无法适应图谱在分布中的变化，造成根本性局限。

Method: 1) 理论分析证明线性注意力在图去噪目标下等价于平均谱滤波器，存在次优性；2) 提出Spectral Attention，直接利用输入图谱实现谱自适应去噪；3) 推导图卷积注意力（GCA），通过图滤波后的查询和键实现谱去噪，满足置换等变性；4) 理论证明softmax操作可近似将噪声特征向量投影到干净特征空间，提供额外去噪效果。

Result: 在Stochastic Block Models上，GCA达到理论最优的Spectral Attention性能。在合成与真实数据集上，用GCA替换线性注意力一致提升图去噪和扩散性能，增益与谱多样性强相关。在DiGress中，GCA无需计算昂贵结构特征即可匹配标准图Transformer性能，结合PEARL位置编码可避免显式特征分解，加速推理且不降质量。

Conclusion: 线性注意力在图去噪中存在根本性局限，GCA通过谱自适应机制有效克服该局限，理论上更优且实践上高效，为图注意力机制的设计提供了新的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Denoising graphs is a fundamental problem in graph learning and the core operation of graph diffusion models. Attention-based architectures like graph transformers have recently shown promise in denoising graphs. However, our principled understanding of attention-based graph denoising remains limited, making it unclear whether standard attention is the right mechanism for this task. Here we show that, under a denoising objective, linear attention is suboptimal and can only learn an average spectral denoising filter over the training distribution. This creates a fundamental limitation as graphs often vary spectrally across the distribution. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Spectral Attention, which directly utilizes the input graph spectrum and provably outperforms linear attention by a margin governed by the spectral diversity of the distribution. We then derive Graph Convolutional Attention (GCA), a practical and permutation-equivariant realization of this idea that implements spectral denoising through graph-filtered queries and keys. For stochastic block models, GCA provably matches the idealized Spectral Attention mechanism. We further show that the softmax operation, that follows the attention, provides additional denoising by approximately projecting noisy eigenvectors onto the clean eigenspace. Empirically, replacing linear attention with GCA consistently improves graph denoising and diffusion on synthetic and real datasets, with gains strongly correlated with spectral diversity. In DiGress, GCA matches standard graph-transformer performance without computing expensive structural features, and when combined with the recently proposed PEARL positional encodings, avoids explicit eigendecomposition computations resulting in faster inference without degrading quality. The code can be found here: github.com/shervinkhalafi/graph_conv_att

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Complets: Universal Compartmentalisation and Programming Model For Arm Permission Overlay Extension 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05569)
*Vasily A. Sartakov*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文介绍了POE2，一种基于内存保护密钥的进程内隔离机制，它通过空间索引（代码和内存的保护密钥）和时间索引（线程上下文属性）来管理访问权限。相比POE1，POE2增强了安全性但增加了硬件复杂性，为此作者提出了一个通用编程模型来抽象其复杂性并支持进程内隔离的软件分区模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POE1（基于内存保护密钥的进程内隔离机制）存在局限性——它不强制执行特权分离，只能作为软件故障隔离技术使用。为了提供更强的安全保障，需要更强大的隔离机制，但随之而来的是架构复杂性的显著增加，导致直接编程困难且容易出错。

Method: 作者首先对POE2进行了详细分析，然后提出了一种通用编程模型，该模型具有强大的安全模型。该模型抽象了空间索引和时间索引的复杂性，同时支持使用进程内隔离构建的分区软件的典型模式。

Result: 提出的编程模型能够抽象POE2的硬件复杂性（空间索引和时间索引的交互），使得开发者可以更简便地利用POE2构建安全的进程内隔离系统，而无需直接处理底层硬件细节。

Conclusion: POE2通过引入时间索引等新机制提供了比POE1更强的安全保证，但架构复杂度也随之增加。作者提出的通用编程模型能够有效抽象这种复杂性，为构建基于POE2的安全进程内隔离系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Arm Permission Overlay Extension (POE) is an intra-process isolation mechanism based on memory protection keys. This mechanism partitions virtual memory into regions whose access permissions can be reconfigured without invoking higher exception levels. Because POE does not enforce privilege separation, POE-based protection domains are applicable only as a software fault-isolation technique.
  The POE2 is an isolation mechanism that substantially extends POE1. Dealing with memory protection keys, it also introduces new registers and tables that regulate the operations permitted for code executing within protection domains associated with different keys. A permission is determined by the spatial index of the executing code (i.e., the protection key associated with code), the spatial index of the accessed memory, the operation type, and a new attribute of the thread context: its temporal index.
  POE2 provides stronger security guarantees than POE1, but also introduces considerable architectural complexity. Effective permissions arise from the interaction of multiple hardware components, making direct programming non-trivial and prone to subtle errors. In the paper, we present a detailed analysis of POE2 and introduce a universal programming model with a strong security model for POE2-based systems. The model abstracts the complexity of spatial and temporal indices while enabling typical patterns of partitioned software constructed using intra-process isolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [BitFair: A 12nm Bit-Serial CNN Accelerator with Learnable Early Termination and Adaptive Bit Ordering for Ultra-Low-Power XR Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05445)
*Ang Li,Chang Gao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BitFair是一种面向XR可穿戴设备的软件-硬件协同设计的位串行CNN加速器，通过可学习的逐层提前终止策略和自适应位排序，在GF 12nm工艺下实现亚毫秒级延迟、117.0 BTOPS/W能效，并在DVS128 Gesture和N-MNIST上分别达到96.5%和97.7%的准确率，能效提升4.0-22.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR可穿戴设备需要在几瓦的功耗和低于20ms的运动到光子延迟预算内实现始终在线的感知，留给神经网络推理的时间仅有几毫秒。现有位串行架构在ReLU输出为零时仍处理所有位，造成能效浪费。

Method: 提出软硬件协同设计的位串行CNN加速器BitFair，包含：(1) 可学习的逐层提前终止策略，通过学习每层阈值，在部分和可靠预测最终ReLU输出为零时提前终止计算；(2) 逐层自适应位排序，优先安排信息量更大的位以最大化提前终止机会而不牺牲精度。

Result: 在GlobalFoundries 12nm FinFET工艺下实现，核心面积0.34mm²，104KB片上存储器，电压范围0.55-0.70V，达到亚毫秒级延迟、117.0 BTOPS/W能效和0.07 pJ/SOP。在IBM DVS128 Gesture和N-MNIST上分别达到96.5%和97.7%的准确率，相比先前的XR视觉加速器，能效提升4.0-22.1倍，准确率提升最高达9.2%。

Conclusion: BitFair通过利用动态位级稀疏性，在严格功耗和延迟约束下显著提升了XR可穿戴设备上CNN推理的能效和准确率，验证了软硬件协同设计在超低功耗边缘AI加速中的有效性。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) wearables require always-on perception within tight power envelopes of a few watts and motion-to-photon latency budgets below 20 ms, leaving only a few milliseconds for neural-network inference. Bit-serial computing is attractive for such energy-efficient neural network acceleration, but many existing architectures still process all bits even when ReLU sets the final output to zero. This paper presents BitFair, a software-hardware co-designed bit-serial CNN accelerator with learnable bit-level early termination and adaptive bit ordering, working under the ultra-low-power and strict latency requirements of XR applications. BitFair exploits dynamic bit-level sparsity by learning per-layer thresholds that trigger early termination when partial sums reliably predict that the final ReLU output will be zero. Furthermore, it searches for layer-wise bit orders that prioritize informative bits, maximizing early termination without sacrificing accuracy. A GlobalFoundries 12nm FinFET implementation with a core area of 0.34 mm^2, 104 KB on-chip memory, and voltage scaling from 0.55 to 0.70 V achieves sub-millisecond latency, up to 117.0 BTOPS/W, and 0.07 pJ/SOP. On IBM DVS128 Gesture and N-MNIST, BitFair achieves 96.5% and 97.7% accuracy, respectively, while improving effective energy efficiency by 4.0-22.1x and accuracy by up to 9.2% over prior fabricated XR vision accelerators.

</details>


### [118] [Neuromorphic Silicon Neuron Controller for Adaptive Deep Brain Stimulation in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05453)
*Md Abu Bakr Siddique,Jakub Orłowski,Yan Zhang,Hongyu An*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于硅神经元（SiLIF-DBS）的闭环自适应深部脑刺激控制器，采用CMOS技术实现，通过β波段生物标志物调控，在抑制病理性β振荡的同时仅消耗开环刺激25%的功耗，实现了5.85%/μW的抑制效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应深部脑刺激（aDBS）方法多停留在算法和软件层面，缺乏面向低功耗可植入集成电路的硬件实现方案。

Method: 采用CMOS工艺实现硅漏积分放电神经元（SiLIF-DBS）控制器，建立其简化计算模型并嵌入帕金森皮层-基底节环路框架进行闭环验证，以β波段STN-LFP的平均整流值（Beta ARV）作为控制生物标志物。

Result: 该控制器在抑制病理性β活动的同时，功耗仅为开环刺激的25%，抑制效率达5.85%/μW。

Conclusion: SiLIF-DBS控制器在低功耗条件下实现了强效的β振荡抑制，为低功耗可植入自适应深部脑刺激提供了可行的硬件基础。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) affects millions worldwide and causes severe motor symptoms. Adaptive deep brain stimulation (aDBS) delivers physiologically informed stimulation that can track fluctuations in PD motor symptoms, enabling more intelligent DBS control. However, most existing aDBS approaches are primarily algorithm- and software-driven, with limited efforts toward circuit realization, particularly low-power and implantable integrated circuits. This paper presents the Silicon Leaky Integrate-and-Fire Deep Brain Stimulation (SiLIF-DBS) controller, a neuromorphic silicon neuron stimulator implemented with metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) technology. For system-level evaluation, a simplified computational model of the SiLIF-DBS controller is derived and embedded within a Parkinsonian cortico-basal ganglia framework for closed-loop validation. The system is driven by beta-band subthalamic nucleus local field potentials (STN-LFPs), with their average rectified value (Beta ARV) used as the control biomarker. Our SiLIF-DBS controller for aDBS suppresses pathological beta activity while consuming only 25% of the power required by open-loop stimulation and achieving a suppression efficiency of $5.85\%$/$μ$W. Overall, our SiLIF-DBS controller achieves strong beta suppression at substantially reduced power, delivering high suppression efficiency that demonstrates it is a viable foundation for low-power implantable aDBS.

</details>


### [119] [Is Your NPU Ready for LLMs? Dissecting the Hidden Efficiency Bottlenecks in Mobile LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05475)
*Guanyu Cai,Ruiming Tian,Lang Yang,Zhouhong Ren,Jinliang Yuan,Lingkun Li,Jiliang Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次对移动端LLM推理进行了跨层测量研究，覆盖5种主流框架和3种硬件后端，开发了PowerBench细粒度剖析工具。研究发现：NPU上框架性能差距可达10倍；NPU擅长计算密集的预填充阶段而CPU擅长内存密集的解码阶段；存在高达40%的能源浪费。据此提出了面向能效的最佳实践配置，可降低NPU后端高达54.8%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备上部署LLM可以增强隐私并降低延迟，但受到硬件效率不足的严重制约。现有研究缺乏对移动LLM推理的全面跨层测量分析，特别是缺乏后端粒度的能耗归因。

Method: 开发了PowerBench细粒度剖析工具，首次实现后端粒度能耗归因。在5个主流框架（llama.cpp、GENIE等）和3种硬件后端（CPU、GPU、NPU）上进行系统测量，分析框架性能差异、阶段拆分特性以及调度开销。

Result: （1）NPU上框架性能差距达10倍；（2）NPU在计算密集的预填充阶段表现出色，CPU在内存密集的解码阶段表现最佳；（3）线程配置、NPU休眠延迟和CPU轮询间隔等调度问题导致高达40%的能源浪费。基于发现提出的配置可降低NPU后端54.8%的能耗。

Conclusion: 移动LLM推理存在显著的跨层效率问题，通过后端感知的调度优化和配置调整，可大幅降低能耗。NPU和CPU在不同推理阶段存在互补优势，应据此进行差异化调度。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on mobile devices enhances privacy and reduces latency, but is severely bottlenecked by hardware inefficiency. We present the first comprehensive, cross-layer measurement study of mobile LLM inference, uniquely spanning five mainstream frameworks (e.g., llama.cpp, GENIE) and three hardware backends (CPU, GPU, NPU). To enable this analysis, we develop PowerBench, a fine-grained profiling tool that provides the first backend-specific energy attribution, moving beyond traditional device-level measurements. Our study yields three critical insights: (1) Framework-induced performance gaps are substantially amplified on NPUs, reaching up to 10x using custom operators due to divergent offloading and quantization strategies. (2) We identify a distinct phase split where NPUs excel at compute-bound prefilling, while CPUs outperform all other backends in memory-bound decoding. This is driven by the NPU's preference for large, fixed-shape workloads, which conflicts with the small-kernel, dynamic nature of decoding. (3) Backend-specific profiling uncovers substantial scheduling headroom missed by prior work. Suboptimal thread configurations, uncoordinated NPU sleep latencies, and CPU polling intervals result in up to 40% energy waste. Leveraging these findings, we present an energy-oriented best-practice configuration for mobile LLM inference. We estimate that this configuration could reduce energy consumption by up to 54.8% on the NPU backend across three datasets.

</details>


### [120] [NEMESIS: NEtlist-Driven Modeling and Equation Synthesis with Inversion-Aware SPICE Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05657)
*Subhadip Ghosh,Ramesh Harjani,Sachin S. Sapatnekar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: NEMESIS是一个多模态框架，利用大语言模型设计运算跨导放大器(OTA)，通过结合快速近似解析模型与精确SPICE评估，在商用65nm工艺下对五种OTA拓扑实现<7%平均相对误差和约4622倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在OTA设计中，纯解析模型速度快但精度不足，而SPICE仿真精度高但计算成本昂贵，现有方法难以兼顾效率与精度，亟需一种能自动平衡两者的框架。

Method: NEMESIS首先识别电路原语，利用LLM从先前相似OTA调用中检索或直接推导初始性能方程，再通过基于SPICE的修复循环迭代精炼方程。

Result: 在商用65nm PDK上，针对五种OTA拓扑，NEMESIS生成SPICE验证的方程，平均相对误差低于7%，收敛后评估速度较纯SPICE评估提升约4622倍。

Conclusion: NEMESIS成功融合LLM的推理能力与SPICE的精确性，在保证高精度的同时实现了数量级的加速，为模拟电路自动化设计提供了有效范式。

Abstract: This work presents NEMESIS, a multimodal framework for operational transconductance amplifier (OTA) design using large language models (LLMs). NEMESIS strikes a balance between fast, approximate analytical models vs. accurate, computationally expensive SPICE evaluations. Given an OTA netlist and schematic, NEMESIS first identifies circuit primitives and then generates progressively more accurate performance equations. The framework begins with equations retrieved from the prior invocations of NEMESIS to structurally similar OTAs, if available; otherwise, it uses the LLM to derive the initial equations directly from the circuit input. These equations are iteratively refined via a SPICE-based repair loop. In a commercial 65nm PDK, NEMESIS is demonstrated on five OTA topologies, producing SPICE-verified equations across biasing ranges with <7% average relative error and a post-convergence evaluation speedup of ~4622x over full SPICE-based evaluation.

</details>


### [121] [Boosting FPGA Performance with Direct BRAM-DSP Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05756)
*Jiajun Hu,Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa,Andrew Boutros,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级FPGA架构增强方案，在BRAM和DSP间引入专用直连通路，并配套改进布局算法，在不影响非DL基准且开销极小的前提下，在Agilex-10类FPGA上实现DL层设计最高+25% Fmax提升和-49%线长缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代FPGA设计中，BRAM与DSP间的数据传输必须经过全局路由网络，导致线长增加、拥塞加剧和关键路径延迟，成为DL工作负载的性能瓶颈。现有近BRAM计算方案需对FPGA架构和CAD工具进行根本性改动，商业可行性受限。

Method: 1) 轻量级架构增强：在BRAM和DSP块之间引入专用直连连接，使BRAM数据无需经过全局互连即可被DSP消费。2) 布局算法增强：修改布局器以识别这些BRAM-DSP宏块对。架构改动开销极小（面积和延迟），CAD保持与基线架构兼容。

Result: 在Agilex-10类FPGA上，对常见DL层设计，Fmax提升最高达+25%，线长缩减最高达-49%。架构改动开销可忽略，不影响非DL基准测试，基线架构上QoR变化可忽略。

Conclusion: 所提BRAM-DSP直连架构增强和配套CAD更新能显著改善DL工作负载的性能瓶颈（Fmax和线长），且开销极小、兼容性好、不影响非DL应用，具有良好的商业可行性。

Abstract: Efficient data movement between memory and compute units is a key performance bottleneck in modern FPGA designs, particularly for deep learning (DL) workloads. In typical FPGA architectures, data transfers between block RAMs (BRAMs) and digital signal processing units (DSPs) must traverse the global routing network, leading to increased wirelength, routing congestion, and critical-path delays. Prior work has explored in- and near-BRAM compute architectures to mitigate these issues, but such solutions often require fundamental changes to FPGA architecture and CAD tools, limiting their commercial viability. This paper proposes a lightweight architectural enhancement that introduces a dedicated direct connection between BRAM and DSP blocks, enabling BRAM data to be consumed by DSPs without passing through the global interconnect. We also enhance the placement algorithm to recognize these BRAM-DSP macro blocks. The proposed architectural change incurs negligible area and delay overhead and does not affect non-DL benchmarks, while the proposed CAD remains compatible with the baseline architecture, where it yields negligible change in quality-of-results (QoR). On an Agilex-10-like FPGA, the proposed architecture and CAD updates deliver up to +25% Fmax and -49% wirelength on common DL layer designs.

</details>


### [122] [GPU-Accelerated Effective Resistance Analysis for 3D IC Power Delivery Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05818)
*Jingchao Hu,Cheng Zhuo,Zhou Jin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种GPU加速的3D IC电源分配网络有效电阻分析框架，相比传统直接求解器实现5-6个数量级的加速，同时保持最大和平均相对误差可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着3D集成中电流需求和设计复杂性增加，电源分配网络面临日益严峻的挑战。TSV的不当规划会导致有效电阻分布不均，进而引发严重的IR Drop问题，因此需要高效的早期阶段PDN有效电阻分析方法。

Method: 提出GPU加速框架用于精确分析早期3D IC PDN的有效电阻，利用GPU并行计算能力加速求解过程。

Result: 相比传统直接求解器实现5-6个数量级的加速，同时最大和平均相对误差保持在可忽略范围内。

Conclusion: 该GPU加速框架能在保证高精度的前提下，极大提升3D IC PDN有效电阻分析的效率，适用于早期设计阶段的快速评估。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) integration is a critical technique for enhancing transistor density, improving power efficiency, and reducing interconnect delays. However, as current demands and design complexity increase, power deliver networks (PDNs) are facing growing challenges.Careful planning of through-silicon vias (TSVs) is essential for ensuring reliable PDNs, where effective resistance serves as a vital metric for the reliability. Ill-planned TSVs often cause 3D IC with unevenly distributed effective resistance and consequently severer IR Drop.In this paper, we propose a GPU-accelerated framework on accurate effective resistance analysis for early stage 3D IC PDNs. The proposed framework achieves a speedup of 5 to 6 orders of magnitude compared to the conventional direct solver, while maintaining negligible deviations in both maximum and average relative errors.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Performance Optimization and Comparative Analysis of Generative AI Models on Advanced Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05400)
*Amitash Nanda,Javier Hernandez Nicolau,Madhusudan Gujral,Mahidhar Tatineni,Amitava Majumdar,Debashis Sahoo*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI模型在异构平台部署时面临的内存、延迟、计算和硬件成本挑战，提出了一种系统的性能优化与对比分析方法，包括混合精度后训练量化评估、微调策略研究，以及在现代HPC系统和先进加速器上的性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型（LLMs和扩散模型）虽性能优异，但部署时面临内存需求大、推理延迟长、计算量高、硬件成本贵等问题，且在不同异构平台（数值格式、内存带宽、软件栈各异）上的评估更加复杂，亟需系统性的优化与对比分析方案。

Method: 采用系统性的性能优化与对比分析方法：提出新型混合精度后训练量化评估方案，研究微调策略，并在现代HPC系统和先进加速器上评估多种生成式AI模型在多样化下游任务中的性能。

Result: 研究提供了混合精度后训练量化、微调策略以及跨异构平台（HPC系统和先进加速器）的性能对比分析结果。

Conclusion: 通过系统性优化与对比分析，有效应对了生成式AI模型在异构平台部署中的内存、延迟、计算和硬件成本挑战。

Abstract: Generative AI models, such as Large Language Models (LLMs) and diffusion models, have demonstrated impressive performance across a wide range of tasks. Despite these advances, deployment remains challenging due to substantial memory requirements, extended inference latency, significant computational demands, and high hardware costs. These issues are further complicated when evaluating models across heterogeneous platforms, where differences in numerical formats, memory bandwidths, and software stacks interact with model architecture and workload characteristics in complex ways. To address these challenges, we present a systematic study focused on performance optimization and comparative analysis of several Generative AI models across diverse downstream tasks. This work introduces a novel mixed-precision post-training quantization evaluation, examines fine-tuning strategies, and assesses performance across modern high-performance computing (HPC) systems and advanced accelerators.

</details>


### [124] [Think Before You Grid-Search: Floor-First Triage for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05876)
*Yihua Liu*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出"Floor First"方法，通过五维资源向量建模和[max, sum]区间分析，实现LLM解码优化的无剖面器预评估，并以DeepSeek-V3.2在H20上的案例验证了该方法在识别资源瓶颈和指导部署决策中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务优化依赖大量基准测试和重型分析器，当延迟目标未达成时优化退化为网格搜索。作者主张反向原则：估计是分析层的前置环节，没有估计则优化退化为网格搜索。

Method: 提出"Floor First"残差驱动的分类工作流。每个解码步骤建模为五维资源向量（HBM字节、FLOPs、网络字节、网络消息、KV容量）；资源内求和、跨资源取最大值得到乐观下限（max），直接求和得到悲观上限（sum）。测量值落在[max, sum]区间内可读出重叠质量，分析器仅在残差超过设定阈值时才升级使用。通过"墙壁排序"（哪个资源墙先绑定）而非点基准比较部署方案。该方法具有组合性，新注意力或状态空间变体只需声明一个模块即可加入。

Result: 以DeepSeek-V3.2风格671B MoE/MLA模型在16块NVIDIA H20 GPU上的案例研究表明：H20的脊点约74 FLOP/byte（H100约590），是极端的解码导向部件。Floot First显示TP16解码受KV容量限制，仅支持约70个并发8K请求；稀疏注意力移除KV带宽项但未突破容量墙；EP16+DP注意力布局接受稍差的同批次权重流量以换取高一个数量级的容量墙（~644），而单流延迟TP优于2.4倍。布局判断是可计算的操作点函数，解释了为何相同硬件的生产部署采用了相反的注意力布局。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为零依赖计算器和代理技能部署，在代理优化循环中强制执行Floor First原则，为LLM服务优化提供了无需剖面器的预评估框架。

Abstract: LLM serving optimization typically benchmarks many configurations and reaches for heavy profilers when latency targets are missed. We argue for the reverse discipline: estimation is the analytical layer of profiling -- without it, optimization degenerates to grid search. Floor First is a residual-driven triage workflow. Each decode step is modeled as a five-dimensional resource vector (HBM bytes, FLOPs, network bytes, network messages, KV capacity); summing within a resource and maximizing across resources gives an optimistic floor, the plain sum a pessimistic one. Where a measurement lands inside this [max, sum] interval reads out overlap quality before any profiler is opened, and profilers escalate only on residuals above a stated threshold. Deployment alternatives are compared by wall ordering -- which resource wall binds first as load grows -- rather than by point benchmarks. The account is compositional: new attention or state-space variants enter by declaring one module, and the workflow ships as a zero-dependency calculator plus an agent skill that enforces the discipline in agentic optimization loops.
  As a case study we analyze a DeepSeek-V3.2-style 671B MoE/MLA model on 16 NVIDIA H20 GPUs, whose ridge point of ~74 FLOP/byte (vs ~590 for H100) makes it an extreme decode-oriented part. The floors show TP16 decoding is KV-capacity-limited to ~70 concurrent 8K requests; sparse attention removes the KV-bandwidth term but not the capacity wall; an EP16+DP-attention layout accepts slightly worse same-batch weight traffic for an order-of-magnitude higher capacity wall (~644) -- while single-stream latency favors TP by 2.4x. The layout judgment is thus a computable function of the operating point, explaining why production deployments on identical hardware have shipped opposite attention layouts.

</details>


### [125] [Energy-Efficient GPU DVFS for Fine-Tuning of SLMs on Resource-constrained Embedded Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05933)
*Jurn-Gyu Park,Sanzhar Zholdybayev,Aidar Amangeldi,Ademi Zhanuzakova*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对资源受限的嵌入式GPU平台上的小语言模型（SLM）微调，研究了DVFS的节能优化。通过表征BERT和Pythia系列模型在GLUE任务上的微调行为，提出了一种基于ML的模型选择方法，在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上相比MAXN Mode 0平均节能13.11%（最高26.73%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护和个性化需求推动小语言模型在本地设备上进行微调，但反复的前向-反向传播比单次推理更耗能。资源受限的嵌入式GPU平台需要高效的DVFS策略来优化能量效率。

Method: 1) 表征了编码器-only（BERT变体）和自回归解码器-only（Pythia变体）SLM在GLUE基准上的微调行为；2) 提出基于机器学习的模型选择方法，自动选择能量最优的GPU DVFS设置。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上，相比无显式功率上限的MAXN Mode 0，平均节能13.11%，最高达26.73%。

Conclusion: 基于ML的DVFS设置选择在嵌入式GPU平台上能有效降低小语言模型微调的能量消耗，验证了该方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic Voltage Frequency Scaling (DVFS) on resource-constrained embedded GPU platforms is essential for energy-efficient small language model (SLM) fine-tuning, as privacy- and personalization-driven adaptation increasingly requires local execution and involves repeated forward-backward optimization over many mini-batches, making it substantially more time- and energy-intensive than single-pass inference. To this end, 1) we first characterize the fine-tuning behavior of representative encoder-only SLMs of BERT variants, and autoregressive decoder-only SLMs of Pythia variants on GLUE benchmarks. In addition to the characterizations, 2) we propose a simple yet effective ML-based model selection that selects energy-optimal GPU DVFS settings on resource-constrained embedded platforms. Our results on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin demonstrate average 13.11% energy savings (up to 26.73%) over MAXN Mode 0, which has no explicit power cap.

</details>


### [126] [A Sub-linear Low-Rank Solver for Poisson's Equation using Machine Learning Frameworks for GPU Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06021)
*Måns I. Andersson,Daniel Appelö*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Cross-DEIM的低秩快速泊松求解器，利用PyTorch在GPU/CPU上实现，通过统计杠杆分数进行索引选择，结合离散正弦变换（DST）和批量FFT，实现了以往不可行的大规模问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统泊松求解器在处理低秩解的大规模问题时计算成本高，且基于DEIM/QDEIM的枢轴算法索引选择代价昂贵，亟需一种高效、可扩展且能利用GPU加速的低秩求解方案。

Method: 采用自适应热启动的Cross-DEIM交叉逼近方法，在索引选择与交叉逼近之间迭代生成低秩解；使用统计杠杆分数替代传统枢轴算法进行索引选择；将交叉逼近与离散正弦变换（DST）泊松求解器自然配对，利用PyTorch实现批量FFT，避免高维全局转置。

Result: 在A100 GPU和AMD EPYC CPU上的性能测试表明，该方法能够处理以往不可行的大规模问题，验证了其实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于PyTorch的Cross-DEIM低秩求解器结合GPU加速和统计杠杆分数索引选择，为大规模泊松问题提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper we explore a fast Poisson solver for problems with a solution that is known to be low-rank. We use an adaptive and warm started cross approximation called Cross-DEIM that iterates between index selection and and cross approximation to generate a low-rank solution. This paper focuses on leveraging a modern machine learning framework, PyTorch, as a general purpose array language to implement low-rank solvers based on Cross-DEIM. PyTorch enables native access to GPUs and accelerators but with a user-friendly high-level interface. We investigate statistical leverage scores for the index selection for the cross approximation due to the cost associated with the pivoted algorithms used with the discrete empirical interpolation methods (DEIM and QDEIM) which are historically preferred. The cross approximation is naturally paired with a Discrete Sine Transform (DST) Poisson solver. This allows the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) to be evaluated in batches along dimensions independently without any global transpose even in higher dimensions. We present performance results running on a A100 GPU and AMD EPYC CPU demonstrating the usefulness of the approach that enables problems sizes that previously were not feasible.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Prompt-to-Paper: Agentic AI System for Bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05456)
*Ramsha Kamran,Maheera Amjad,Zartasha Mustansar,Arsalan Shaukat,Salma Sherbaz,Muhammad U. S. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Prompt-to-Paper多智能体框架，通过确定性检索增强生成、自治编码实验执行和八维质量评分器三大创新，在零超范围引用下以约0.31美元/篇的成本生成可发表级别的手稿，质量评分平均提升+17.96分（满分100），人工评审平均7.0/10分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在端到端自动手稿生成中存在三个关键缺陷：生成声明无法确定性溯源至可验证文献、实验结果常被编造而非真实执行、缺乏标准化多维框架评估AI生成手稿是否达到实际发表所需的质量和严谨性。

Method: 提出了Prompt-to-Paper框架，包含三个核心创新：(1) 确定性检索增强生成流水线，采用分节感知相关性评分和雪球引用扩展，将每个声明锚定在60-100篇可验证文献中；(2) 自治编码代理执行真实计算生物学实验，用真实数值结果替代合成输出；(3) 八维自动质量评分器，以已发表论文的近似参考统计为基准，加入显式幻觉惩罚，提供标准化可复现的质量评估。质量驱动改进循环通过上下文丰富的修订器每十次迭代触发深度研究循环以重新运行实验和重写手稿。

Result: 在五个生物信息学案例研究上验证，所有五例均生成提交格式的PDF且零超范围引用。改进循环使手稿质量在0-100分尺度上平均提升+17.96分（最高+26.04分）。人工评审员对五篇手稿平均评分为7.0/10分。完整手稿生产成本约0.31美元/篇。

Conclusion: Prompt-to-Paper框架通过确定性文献溯源、真实实验执行和自动化质量评估，有效解决了AI生成手稿的可验证性和质量控制问题，以极低成本产出达到人工评审标准（7/10分）的学术手稿。

Abstract: While recent advances in large language models have enabled end-to-end automated manuscript generation, existing systems suffer from three critical deficiencies: (i) generated claims are not deterministically grounded in verifiable literature, (ii) experimental results are frequently fabricated rather than executed, and (iii) there exists no standardized, multi-dimensional framework to assess whether AI-generated manuscripts meet the quality and rigor required for real-world publication. We present Prompt-to-Paper, a multi-agent framework that directly addresses this evaluation gap through three integrated innovations. First, a deterministic retrieval-augmented generation pipeline with section-aware relevance scoring and snowball citation expansion grounds every claim in a verifiable corpus of 60--100 papers. Second, an autonomous coding agent executes real computational biology experiments replacing synthetic outputs with genuine numerical results. Third, an eight-dimensional automated quality scorer, benchmarked with approximate reference statistics from published papers and augmented with explicit hallucination penalties, provides standardized, reproducible quality assessments. The quality-driven improvement loop uses a context-rich reviser that routes each iteration to one of three researcher actions and fires a deep research cycle every ten iterations to re-run experiments and re-manuscript from stronger outputs. We validate the system on five bioinformatics case studies; all five cases compiled submission-formatted PDFs with zero out-of-range citations. The improvement loop raises manuscript quality by an average of +17.96 points on a 0--100 scale (maximum +26.04. As partial external checks, a human reviewer scored the five manuscripts at an average of 7.0 out of 10. Complete manuscripts are produced at approximately 0.31 USD per paper.

</details>


### [128] [From Graphs to Gradients: Physics-Inspired Structural Attribution for Cyber-Physical IoT Systems and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05563)
*Spyridon Evangelatos,Christos Diou,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos,Evangelos Markakis,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于统计力学能量模型的框架，通过无向能量表示对网络物理物联网系统中的变量依赖关系建模，实现不依赖有向因果图的归因解释，在工业物联网仿真中相比图方法展现出更高准确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释AI方法主要捕捉输入输出间的相关性，而因果解释需恢复有向因果结构，这在具有反馈回路和部分可观测性的大规模混合网络物理系统中往往不可行。因此需要一种不依赖显式有向因果图的解释方法。

Method: 受统计力学启发，通过基于能量的无向表示对网络物理物联网系统中的变量依赖关系建模；通过分析能量景观的变化来反映单个组件的影响，实现依赖感知的归因；支持跨混合交互的扰动效应推理。

Result: 在包含混合连续和离散变量的工业物联网测试台上进行仿真实验，证明该框架比最先进的基于图的方法具有更高的归因准确性、更好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法虽不旨在完全恢复系统的生成动力学，但能提供有价值的依赖感知解释，支持人工理解和下游预测/诊断任务；除工业物联网安全外，还可应用于其他高维网络物理和社会技术系统。

Abstract: Interpretable explanation methods in Artificial Intelligence aim to uncover the underlying causes and their effects, enabling a deeper understanding of why a system behaves in a certain way under different inputs. Unlike traditional explainability methods, which mainly highlight correlations between input and output variables, causal explanation focuses on interventional questions. By doing so, it provides more robust insights, helping users understand automated decisions, especially in high-risk domains. Recovering an explicit directed causal structure, however, is often impractical in large-scale, hybrid cyber-physical systems with feedback loops and partial observability. This paper introduces a novel framework inspired by statistical mechanics that instead models variable dependencies through an undirected, energy-based representation of cyber-physical IoT systems. Our approach enables rigorous dependency-aware attribution by analysing how variations in the energy landscape reflect the influence of individual components, without recovering a directed causal graph. It also supports reasoning about perturbation effects across hybrid interactions, providing reliable explanations of abnormal behaviours. We empirically examined our framework through simulations on an industrial IoT testbed with hybrid continuous and discrete variables, demonstrating higher attribution accuracy, improved robustness and better scalability than state-of-the-art graph-based approaches. While the attributions are not intended to fully recover the system's generative dynamics, they provide valuable, dependency-aware explanations supporting both human interpretation and downstream predictive and diagnostic tasks. Although demonstrated in industrial IoT security, our framework also applies to other high-dimensional cyber-physical and socio-technical systems requiring principled, structural explanations.

</details>


### [129] [CSTutorBench: Benchmarking Small Language Models as Tutors for Block-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05571)
*H. Chad Lane,Bryson Kageler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CSTutorBench基准，用于评估语言模型在基于块的编程环境VEX VR中作为CS导师的表现。初步发现模型在表面标准上表现良好，但在深层教学行为上（如避免答案泄露、处理学生调试历史）存在困难；模型家族和指令微调方法比参数量更能预测教学质量；针对性的提示修订改进了多数模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为AI导师在K-12教育中部署存在隐私、成本和依赖专有模型等问题。小语言模型（SLM）是替代方案，但针对特定教育场景（如基于块的编程）选择合适的模型仍然困难，因为这些领域在模型训练数据中基本不存在。

Method: 构建了包含17个基于场景的问题的CSTutorBench基准，基于教学和反馈研究成果制定教学评估量规，采用人机协同的LLM-as-judge流水线进行评估。对11个模型（4B-120B参数）进行测试，并基于教育提示工程研究进行针对性的提示修订。

Result: 模型在词汇和语气等表面标准上表现良好，但在避免答案泄露和与学生的调试历史互动等深层教学行为上表现不佳。模型家族和指令微调方法比参数量更能预测教学质量。针对性的提示修订改进了11个模型中的10个。

Conclusion: 情境特定、以教学为基础的教育基准对于SLM在教育部署中的选择具有重要价值。模型家族和指令微调方法对教学质量的预测优于参数量。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly explored as AI tutors, yet deploying them in K-12 settings raises concerns around privacy, cost, and reliance on proprietary models. Small language models (SLMs) offer a promising alternative, but selecting the right model for a specific educational context remains difficult, particularly when the target domain, such as block-based programming, is largely absent from model training data. We introduce CSTutorBench, a benchmark for evaluating language models as CS tutors in VEX VR, a block-based robotics environment. The benchmark comprises 17 scenario-based questions scored against a pedagogical rubric grounded in established tutoring and feedback research, with a human-in-the-loop LLM-as-judge pipeline for evaluation. Preliminary findings across 11 models (4B-120B parameters) reveal that models perform well on surface-level criteria such as vocabulary and tone but struggle with deeper pedagogical behaviors, particularly avoiding answer leakage and engaging with student debugging histories. In our sample, model family and instruction-tuning approach appear to be better predictors of tutoring quality than parameter count alone, though the small number of models limits the strength of this conclusion. A targeted prompt revision grounded in recent educational prompt engineering research improved scores for 10 of 11 models. These results underscore the value of context-specific, pedagogically grounded benchmarks for SLM selection in educational deployment.

</details>


### [130] [Auto-DSM Under the Lens: A Black-Box Evaluation Framework for LLM-Based DSM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05985)
*Niels Potters,Theo Hofman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个黑盒评估框架，系统性地评估大语言模型从结构化技术文档中生成设计结构矩阵的能力，通过结构指标、分类指标和稳定性指标的综合评分，在虚构系统和真实冰箱分解数据集上验证了LLM生成DSM的潜力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动DSM管线多为闭源，缺乏可重复的评估方法，因此需要建立一个透明的基准测试框架来审计LLM驱动的DSM生成能力。

Method: 提出黑盒评估框架，结合单次运行与多次运行视角，集成结构指标（完整性、正确性、耦合密度）、分类指标（选择性准确率、弃权覆盖率）和稳定性指标（熵、Fleiss' κ），并设计复合质量评分Q。在虚构抽象系统和真实冰箱分解两个数据集上进行控制实验，涵盖措辞、参数-数据集对齐和系统复杂度变化。

Result: LLM能生成结构上合理的DSM，在输入结构良好的条件下具有高可重复性，但对歧义、不一致的依赖定义和提示措辞敏感；发现了系统性的幻觉和弃权失败来源。

Conclusion: 该框架为审计自动DSM管线提供了透明基准，为将基于LLM的分解方法集成到基于模型的系统工程工作流中奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a black-box evaluation framework to systematically assess the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate Design Structure Matrices (DSMs) from structured technical documentation. Motivated by the closed-source nature of current Auto-DSM pipelines, the framework introduces a reproducible methodology that benchmarks generated DSMs (GEN-DSMs) against manually validated ground-truth matrices (GT-DSMs). The evaluation integrates both single-run and multi-run perspectives, combining structural metrics (Completeness, Correctness, Coupling Density), classification metrics (Selective Accuracy, Abstention Coverage), and stability measures (Entropy, Fleiss' $κ$). To synthesize these aspects, a Composite Quality Score (Q) is proposed. Controlled experiments are conducted on two datasets: a fictive abstract system and a real-world refrigerator decomposition, covering variations in phrasing, parameter-dataset alignment, and system complexity. Results show that LLMs can produce structurally plausible DSMs and achieve high reproducibility under well-structured inputs, but remain sensitive to ambiguity, inconsistent dependency definitions, and prompt formulation. The findings highlight systematic sources of hallucination and abstention failure, demonstrating both the potential and current limitations of LLM-driven DSM automation. The proposed framework provides a transparent benchmark for auditing Auto-DSM pipelines and establishes foundations for integrating LLM-based decomposition methods into model-based systems engineering (MBSE) workflows.

</details>


### [131] [Foundation Models for Automatic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05573)
*J de Curtò,Victoria Guillén,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMForge框架，结合JSON校验、分析评分、网格合成与多轮迭代优化，在97个工程设计的基准上评估7个基础模型。IterTracer使用光线追踪渲染与分析视觉指标，IterVision用VLM语义批评器替代分析评分器。实验表明紧凑型指令微调模型可达与更大系统相当的性能，VLM批评器在旋转对称几何体上存在视觉与语义评分分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型和视觉语言模型能否从自然语言描述自动生成参数化3D机械设计，并统一评估不同基础模型在实际工程设计问题上的表现。

Method: 提出LLMForge多模型文本转CAD框架，包含JSON结构校验、分析特征评分、网格合成和多轮迭代优化。设计两种批评机制：IterTracer使用Phong着色光线追踪渲染器结合分析视觉指标（轮廓IoU、孔可见性、边距、宽高比一致性）；IterVision用Qwen2.5-VL-72B视觉语言模型进行链式思维视觉推理评估。在97个问题、4类几何族（带孔和螺栓圆的板、多特征盒、法兰圆柱、L形支架）上评估7个模型。

Result: IterTracer下前4名模型紧密聚类（总体均值[0.885,0.890]），网格成功率达98.97%，表明紧凑指令微调模型可匹配更大系统。IterVision中领先模型实现100%水密网格生成，但在圆柱等旋转对称几何体上视觉与语义评分分歧最大。

Conclusion: 紧凑型指令微调模型在机械CAD生成任务上可达到与大型系统相当的性能；VLM批评器虽提升水密性，但在旋转对称几何体上存在评估分歧；研究对基准设计、故障模式、CAD提示工程及工业工作流可扩展自动化机械设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable the automatic generation of parametric 3D designs from natural-language specifications. This chapter presents an empirical study of foundation models for automatic Computer-Aided Design (CAD) generation of mechanical parts, using a unified evaluation pipeline and a curated benchmark of 97 engineering design problems. We introduce LLMForge, a multi-model text-to-CAD framework integrating JSON-schema validation, analytic feature scoring, mesh synthesis, and multi-round iterative refinement, studied under two critique regimes. IterTracer uses a Phong-shaded ray-trace renderer with analytic visual metrics (silhouette IoU, hole visibility, edge clearance, aspect-ratio conformance) for lightweight geometry-aware feedback across rounds. IterVision replaces the analytic scorer with a VLM semantic critic (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) that evaluates rendered views via chain-of-thought visual reasoning, assessing spatial coherence and design intent. On a benchmark spanning four canonical geometry families (plates with holes and bolt circles, multi-feature boxes, flanged cylinders, and L-brackets), we evaluate seven foundation models: DeepSeek-V3.2, Qwen3-235B-A22B, Llama-3.3-70B, Gemma-3-27B, GLM-4.5, MiniMax-M2.1, and INTELLECT. Under IterTracer, the four highest-ranked models form a tight cluster (overall mean in [0.885, 0.890]) with 98.97% mesh success, showing that compact instruction-tuned models can match substantially larger systems. VLM-based critique in IterVision yields 100% watertight mesh generation on the leading model while surfacing systematic difficulty on rotationally symmetric geometries such as cylinders, where visual and semantic scoring diverge most. We discuss benchmark design, failure modes, CAD-oriented prompting, and implications for industrial workflows and scalable automated mechanical design.

</details>


### [132] [Narrative World Model: Narratology-Grounded Writer Memory for Long-Form Fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05577)
*Mohammad Saifullah,Thomas Kornmaier,Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma,Aditya Sanjiv Kanade,Aanand Kumar Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出叙事世界模型(NWM)，一个面向长篇小说写作者的内存系统，通过结合叙事学 grounding 的时序状态图与查询条件混合检索，显著优于现有时间知识图谱代理内存框架(Graphiti/Zep)在多跳叙事QA任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇小说写作者需要回答关于故事状态演化的多跳问题(如谁在何时知道某个秘密、事件是否发生在揭示该事件的叙述之前等)，而通用的检索和代理内存系统仅表示实体和事实，缺乏叙事学结构，导致检索到错误证据或无法找到证据。

Method: NWM由两部分组成：(1) 基于叙事学 grounding 的类型化时序状态图；(2) 查询条件混合检索。评估时通过固定的 Opus 4.8 阅读器仅读取系统提供的章节级安全证据，以衡量内存质量而非回答能力。

Result: 在可复现的公开语料库和经过验证的多跳基准测试中，NWM大幅且显著优于最强基线 Graphiti/Zep，并远超 GraphRAG 和扁平检索。优势源于其叙事学 grounding 的结构和查询条件检索，而非图大小或抽取器质量。

Conclusion: 叙事学 grounding 的类型化时序状态图结构与查询条件混合检索是提升长篇小说多跳叙事问答的关键，NWM在表征层面而非抽取层面取得了实质性优势。

Abstract: Long-form fiction writers need memory that answers multi-hop questions about evolving story state: who knows a secret and when they learned it, whether an event preceded the narration that revealed it, whether a setup paid off, and how a relationship shifted. General-purpose retrieval and agent-memory systems represent entities and facts but not the narratological structure these questions turn on, so they surface the wrong evidence or none at all. We introduce the Narrative World Model (NWM), a writer-memory system that pairs a narratology-grounded typed temporal-state graph with query-conditioned hybrid retrieval. To measure memory rather than the answerer, we read every system through a single held-constant Opus 4.8 reader over only that system's chapter-safe evidence, on a reproducible public corpus and a validated multi-hop benchmark, and we compare against the strongest existing temporal-knowledge-graph agent-memory framework, Graphiti/Zep (Rasmussen et al., 2025). NWM substantially and significantly outperforms this baseline on multi-hop narratological QA across both corpora, and far exceeds GraphRAG and flat retrieval. The advantage is representational rather than an artifact of extraction: it survives rebuilding the baseline with NWM's own extractor, and traces to its narratology-grounded structure and query-conditioned retrieval, not to graph size or extractor quality.

</details>


### [133] [FirstResearch: Auditable Question Formation for LLM Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05682)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FirstResearch框架，通过结构化的研究问题证书（包含原始定义、假设、机制模型、矛盾、可证伪假设、关键实验和失败更新规则），使LLM生成的科学问题可审计。实验表明该框架在可审计性上优于多个基线，证书组件是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统在科学发现中提出的第一个研究问题难以审计——它可能听起来合理但未暴露机制、可证伪条件或假设，科学家难以验证其有效性。

Method: 提出FirstResearch框架，核心是结构化研究问题证书（Research Question Certificate），包含原始定义、假设、机制模型、张力/矛盾、可证伪假设、最小决定性实验和失败更新规则。在10个LLM智能体研究主题上，基于DeepSeek盲评和Gemini独立重评进行对比实验。

Result: FirstResearch在DeepSeek盲评协议下优于所有基线；Gemini重评保持相同排名（4.86/5 vs 最强基线4.38/5），Pearson一致性0.865。消融实验显示仅证书组件得分4.90/5（DeepSeek）和4.88/5（Gemini），移除证书后得分低于1/5。

Conclusion: 显式推导约束是使LLM生成科学问题更可审计的有效机制。本研究虽为初步结果且使用LLM评判而非人类专家，但支持这一窄科学发现主张。

Abstract: LLM systems for scientific discovery increasingly assist with ideation, literature synthesis, experiment planning, and report generation, but the first research question they propose can remain difficult to audit: it may sound plausible without exposing the mechanism, falsifier, or assumption that a scientist should inspect. We introduce FirstResearch, a first-principles research-question formation framework for scientific LLM agents whose core artifact is a structured Research Question Certificate. The certificate records primitive definitions, assumptions, a mechanism model, a tension or contradiction, a falsifiable hypothesis, a minimal decisive test, and a failure update rule, making the proposed question inspectable before downstream execution. On ten LLM-agent research topics, FirstResearch outperforms controlled prompt-level baselines inspired by AI co-scientist, Agent Laboratory, and AI Scientist-v2 under a primary DeepSeek-blind-judge protocol. A Gemini-2.5-Flash independent-judge rescore of the same 40 baseline packages preserves the system-level ranking, with FirstResearch scoring 4.86/5 versus 4.38/5 for the strongest baseline and Pearson agreement of 0.865 on average score. A one-repeat ablation checkpoint further suggests that the certificate-centered core is the strongest component: certificate-only scoring reaches 4.90/5 under DeepSeek and 4.88/5 under Gemini, while removing certificates drops below 1/5 under both judges. These results are preliminary and use LLM judges rather than human domain experts, but they support a narrow scientific-discovery claim: explicit derivation constraints are a promising mechanism for making LLM-generated scientific questions more auditable. Code, prompts, saved outputs, and reproduction scripts are available at https://github.com/louiswang524/FirstResearch.

</details>


### [134] [Memory in the Loop: In-Process Retrieval as ExtendedWorking Memory for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05690)
*Yusuf Khan,Carlo Lipizzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将记忆存储从循环外部移入语言模型智能体的循环内部，利用进程内存储（~100μs）替代网络存储（数十毫秒），将每步记忆读写成本降低三个数量级，从而显著减少冗余动作并提升事实召回率。实验表明，在固定记忆延迟预算下，冗余动作随延迟单调增加（进程内速度下0.0/12，云端往返110ms下7.2/12）；在四种GPT-5类模型上，循环内记忆将召回率从0/5提升至3.6-4.8/5，且所有写入（244/244）均未丢失，瓶颈从存储转移至嵌入层（网络嵌入200-400ms vs 本地嵌入~40μs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言智能体将记忆存储置于循环外部，每回合最多查询一次，导致延迟成为障碍（网络存储延迟数十至数百毫秒，端到端延迟可增加83倍）。现有方法通过调度或限制检索来管理成本，而非质疑这一模式本身。本文旨在论证延迟是存储位置而非循环模式的属性，通过将存储移入进程内实现每步读写成本骤降，使记忆从外部工具转变为扩展工作记忆。

Method: 1) 对比实验：固定每回合记忆延迟预算，仅改变存储响应速度（进程内~100μs vs 云端110ms），测量冗余动作数量。2) 端到端评估：在四种GPT-5类模型上，使用有界窗口+循环内记忆，测试事实召回率。3) 瓶颈分析：分离存储操作与嵌入操作的延迟贡献，对比网络嵌入（200-400ms）与本地小嵌入器（~40μs）的性能。

Result: 1) 冗余动作随延迟单调递增：进程内速度下0.0/12个冗余动作，云端110ms往返下7.2/12个（精确排列检验p=0.0079）。2) 循环内记忆将召回率从0/5提升至3.6-4.8/5（四种GPT-5类模型），存储操作p50延迟为80-165μs。3) 244次写入全部保留，无事实丢失；所有缺失均归因于智能体读取策略而非存储。4) 配对本地小嵌入器后，完整操作降至~40μs。

Conclusion: 进程内存储使每步记忆访问成本从网络延迟（数十毫秒）降至微秒级，消除了循环内记忆的延迟障碍，使记忆从外部工具转变为智能体的扩展工作记忆。这一范式不仅显著提升事实召回率、减少冗余动作，还将瓶颈从存储转移至嵌入层，为未来构建完全在内存中运行的智能体架构提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Language agents run a loop - observe, reason, act - but the memory they reason over sits outside it: a store queried at most once per turn. We study the regime where memory moves inside the loop, read and written on every step. The obstacle has always been latency: networked stores answer in tens to hundreds of milliseconds, and in-loop retrieval can inflate end-to-end latency by up to 83x when retrieval is expensive. Prior work manages that cost rather than questioning it: serving-layer scheduling hides it, "memory-first" designs ration retrieval to once per turn. We argue latency is a property of where the store lives, not the in-loop pattern: an in-process store answers in ~100us, three orders of magnitude below the network regime, and at that speed the per-step tax collapses. By the extended-mind thesis's parity principle, a store fast enough to be constantly and directly available becomes extended working memory, not a tool the agent merely consults. The premise is causal: holding a fixed per-turn memory-latency budget and varying only the store's answer speed, redundant actions rise monotonically with latency - 0.0 of 12 at in-process speed, 7.2 of 12 at a 110ms cloud round trip (gpt-5-nano, gpt-5-mini; exact permutation p=0.0079). We demonstrate the regime end-to-end: across four GPT-5-class models under a bounded window, recall improves from 0/5 to 3.6-4.8/5 with in-loop memory, store ops at p50 80-165us - though an instructed restate-every-reply baseline also solves it perfectly, at a token cost that grows with the working set. The store never lost a fact in any run (244 of 244 writes kept); every miss traces to the agent's read policy, not the store. Our measurements also relocate the bottleneck: the dominant per-step cost is embedding (~200-400ms over the network); pairing the in-process store with a small local embedder returns the complete operation to a measured ~40us.

</details>


### [135] [Akashic: A Low-Overhead LLM Inference Service with MemAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05708)
*Yang Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Huayi Jin,Ruozhou He,Chenchen Hong,Zhiyong Wang,Yifei Liu,Yunfei Gu,Chentao Wu,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Akashic是一个基于MemAttention的低开销记忆系统，通过将上下文组织为有界块并建模块间语义关系，配合软硬件协同设计的内存布局，在降低开销的同时提升任务准确性、吞吐量和可持续请求率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理系统在多轮交互、工具调用和跨会话工作流中不断累积上下文，回放完整历史导致预填充成本高、易超上下文限制、任务相关证据被无关内容淹没，影响服务效率和输出质量。

Method: 提出Akashic系统，核心是MemAttention机制：将上下文组织为有界块（bounded chunks）并建模块间语义关系，保留跨块证据而无需重复重写完整历史；同时采用软硬件协同设计的记忆放置策略，将可能共检索的块就近放置，减少检索碎片化和I/O开销。

Result: 在四个代表性工作负载和三个模型规模上，相比强基线记忆系统，任务准确率提升最高10.2个百分点，吞吐量提升最高1.21倍，可持续请求率提升最高1.88倍。

Conclusion: Akashic通过有界块化组织、语义关系建模和软硬件协同内存布局，有效解决了长上下文场景下的效率与质量问题，是一种低开销、高收益的LLM代理记忆系统方案。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based agent systems continuously accumulate context across multi-turn interactions, tool invocations, and cross-session workflows. Replaying the full history for every request quickly becomes impractical: long contexts increase prefill cost, may exceed context limits, and often bury task-relevant evidence in irrelevant content, degrading both serving efficiency and output quality. We propose Akashic, a low-overhead memory system built around MemAttention, which organizes context into bounded chunks and models semantic relationships across chunks, preserving cross-chunk evidence without repeatedly rewriting the full history. Akashic further applies hardware-software co-designed memory placement to co-locate likely co-retrieved chunks, reducing retrieval fragmentation and I/O overhead. Across four representative workloads and three model sizes, Akashic improves task accuracy by up to 10.2 points, throughput by up to 1.21x, and sustainable request rate by up to 1.88x over strong prior memory baselines.

</details>


### [136] [ArtisanCAD: An Industrial-Level CAD Agent with Expert-Grounded Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05750)
*Yunhan Xu,Qifeng Wu,Xunjin Li,Yuanwei Bin,Qingsong Yao,Jianghang Gu,Guan Wang,Weihao Lv,Huiyu Yang,Wenfa Luo,Jiao Xiang,Yuntian Chen,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 \algname，一种基于技能引导的工业CAD智能体，通过CAD中间表示（CAD-IR）将专家CAD流程蒸馏为可复用的参数化技能，并支撑从模糊文本意图到可执行CATIA操作的完整生成流程，在Text2CAD基准和复杂汽车部件任务上显著提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD方法在处理模糊、欠指定或仅描述高层设计意图的用户提示时效果不佳，且很少利用工业工作流中自然存在的专家过程知识（如CATIA操作记录、宏日志、工程描述等）。

Method: 提出CAD-IR（CAD中间表示），一种可执行的过程式表示，编码参数、有序操作、MCP工具绑定、依赖关系、生成实体及验证规则；通过CAD-IR将专家CAD流程蒸馏为可复用参数化技能，并为模糊提示提供过程化支架；结合专家技能检索、CAD-IR实例化与修正、CATIA-MCP后端执行及多视角视觉反馈迭代优化，最终生成生产级B-Rep模型。

Result: 在Text2CAD基准上，CAD-IR将中间提示的生成平均Chamfer Distance从14.83降至9.88；在四个复杂汽车零部件上，成功将专家CATIA记录蒸馏为可复用技能，并生成可编辑的CATIA原生B-Rep模型。

Conclusion: CAD-IR能有效弥合模糊文本意图与可执行CAD构建之间的鸿沟，并将专家过程知识转化为可复用的技能，支撑工业级CAD智能体的构建。

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) for industrial components requires long-horizon procedural modeling, robust feature dependencies, editable parametric geometry, and production-grade B-Rep execution. Existing text-to-CAD methods have made promising progress in generating CAD programs from natural-language descriptions, but they still struggle when user prompts are ambiguous, underspecified, or only describe high-level design intent. They also rarely exploit expert procedural knowledge naturally available in industrial workflows, such as CATIA operation recordings, macro logs, drawing notes, and engineering descriptions. We present \algname, a skill-guided industrial CAD agent with expert-grounded knowledge distillation. The core of \algname is CAD intermediate representation (CAD-IR), an executable procedural representation that encodes parameters, ordered operations, MCP tool bindings, dependencies, generated entities, and verification rules. CAD-IR plays two key roles: it first serves as the carrier for distilling expert CAD procedures into reusable parameterized skills; then it provides a procedural scaffold that turns vague or intermediate-level prompts into complete executable CAD operations. \algname retrieves expert-derived skills, instantiates and revises CAD-IR, executes the resulting procedure through a dedicated CATIA-MCP backend, and uses multi-view visual feedback for iterative refinement, and finally generates production-ready B-Rep models. On the Text2CAD benchmark, CAD-IR improves generation from intermediate prompts by reducing mean Chamfer Distance from $14.83$ to $9.88$, showing its ability to bridge ambiguous textual intent and executable CAD construction. On four complex automotive components, CAD-IR enables expert CATIA recordings to be distilled into reusable skills, allowing \algname to generate editable CATIA-native B-Rep models for new variant requests.

</details>


### [137] [Synthetic Consumer Insight Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05761)
*Stephen L. France,Pia. A. Albinsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用大语言模型（LLM）生成投射技术所需的合成消费者数据，发现LLM与人类在广泛主题和联想上有显著重叠，但在风格、语言结构和多样性生成方式上存在重要差异，并给出了最佳实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统消费者数据收集成本高、耗时长且难以规模化，而投射技术作为挖掘消费者内在联想、情感和需求的方法，同样面临这些挑战。研究旨在探索LLM能否作为一种经济高效的替代方案来生成合成消费者数据。

Method: 在多组投射任务中，使用不同的LLM、提示策略和温度设置生成响应，并与针对城市旅游目的地感知的真实人类调查数据进行对比。通过语言学指标、多样性和集中度指标、主题模型以及高频词分析进行多维比较。

Result: LLM与人类响应在宏观主题和联想层面存在大量重叠，但在语言风格、句法结构和多样性生成的内在机制上表现出显著差异。不同模型和提示策略会显著影响响应质量。

Conclusion: LLM可用于生成合成消费者数据，但需认识到其在语言风格和多样性方面的局限性，应根据具体需求合理选择模型、提示策略和参数设置。

Abstract: Modern data-driven marketing relies on large amounts of consumer data, yet collecting such data can be costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. This research examines whether large language models (LLMs) can be used to generate synthetic consumer data for projective techniques, a set of methods designed to elicit consumer associations, emotions, wants, and needs. We test LLM-generated responses across multiple projective tasks, LLMs, prompting strategies, and temperature settings, and compare them with human responses from a primary research study on perceptions of city tourism destinations. Human and LLM responses were analyzed using linguistic measures, diversity and concentration metrics, topic models, and top-term analyses. The results show substantial overlap between human and LLM responses in broad topics and associations, but also important differences in style, linguistic structure, and the way diversity is generated. Recommendations are given on how to best utilize LLMs for generating synthetic consumer data, how model and prompt choices shape response quality, and on recognizing the limitations of LLM synthetic consumer data generation.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond Static Evaluation: Building Simulation Environments for Scalable Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05773)
*Akshay Arora,Ishan Nigam,Ashutosh Aggarwal,Shefali Bansal,Krishna Singh,Sweta Kumari,Nikhil Mittal,Shariq Farhan,Siddarth Malreddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgenticAI-Supervisor，一个通过API和UI驱动的RL Gym环境，将环境创建与可扩展执行解耦，利用可验证执行结果生成高保真轨迹并应用多维奖励塑形，通过内部状态验证缓解奖励黑客攻击，并在客服Agent案例中展示了闭环反馈优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型进化为自主智能体后，传统的静态评估方法无法捕捉多步决策过程，需要新的评估框架。

Method: 设计了AgenticAI-Supervisor平台：API和UI驱动的RL Gym环境，解耦环境创建与可扩展执行；基于可验证执行结果生成高保真轨迹；应用多维奖励塑形；通过内部状态验证和测试缓解奖励黑客攻击。

Result: 以客服Agent案例研究展示了平台核心能力，实现了模型优化的持续闭环反馈。

Conclusion: 该平台为LLM智能体的多步决策评估提供了可扩展的解决方案，未来工作将聚焦计算机使用、工具使用、自动"难例生成"和边缘案例生成等高级功能。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, traditional static evaluation fails to capture multi-step decision-making. We introduce AgenticAI-Supervisor, an API and UI-driven RL Gym environment that decouples environment creation from scalable execution. By moving to verifiable execution outcomes, the platform generates high-fidelity traces and applies multi-dimensional reward shaping. Critically, our framework mitigates reward hacking through rigorous internal state validation and testing. This work provides a first look at our platform's core capabilities through a Customer Support Agent case study demonstrating a consistent closed-loop feedback for model optimization. Future work will focus on advanced features such as Computer Use, Tool Use, automated "stumping", and edge-case generation.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond the Leaderboard: A Synthesis of Tool-Use, Planning, and Reasoning Failures in Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05775)
*Wael Albayaydh,Rui Zhao,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综合27篇论文（2023-2026年）和19个基准，提出了首个跨工具使用、规划、长程推理、多智能体协作、安全性和测量有效性等维度的统一LLM智能体缺陷分类法，识别出6个失败簇：工具调用错误、规划与约束失败、长程退化、多智能体协作失败、安全漏洞和测量有效性问题。研究发现失败随任务长度非线性累积，子任务强性能不保证端到端成功，额外脚手架无法持续提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估工作分散，不同基准报告的成功常掩盖重复出现的失败模式，缺乏统一的跨维度缺陷分类体系来系统理解这些失败的根本原因。

Method: 综合27篇基准测试、分类法和审计论文（2023-2026年），涵盖19个不同基准，通过迭代方式将独立报告的各类错误按智能体推理-行动管线的不同阶段归类为主题，形成跨领域的统一分类法。

Result: 识别出6个失败簇：1) 工具调用与参数级错误；2) 规划与约束满足失败；3) 上下文积累导致的长程退化；4) 多智能体协作失败；5) 对抗或欠指定条件下的安全与安保失败；6) 测量有效性问题。发现失败随任务长度非线性加剧，子任务强性能不保证端到端成功，额外脚手架（scaffolding）无法持续提升可靠性。

Conclusion: 单轮工具使用、短程网页导航和窄范围编码任务已取得显著进展，但长程、复杂场景下的可靠性仍是核心挑战，需更系统的评估和方法论改进。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly evaluated on their ability to use tools, plan multi-step tasks, coordinate with other agents, and operate over extended horizons. Reported benchmark gains often obscure recurring failure modes documented across otherwise unrelated evaluation efforts. This paper synthesizes 27 benchmark, taxonomy, and audit papers (2023-2026), spanning 19 distinct benchmarks, into a cross-cutting taxonomy of agent limitations. To our knowledge, this is the first synthesis that integrates evidence across tool use, planning, long-horizon reasoning, multi-agent coordination, safety, and measurement validity into a single, unified taxonomy of LLM agent limitations. We identify six failure clusters: (1) tool invocation and parameter-level errors, (2) planning and constraint-satisfaction failures, (3) long-horizon degradation from context accumulation, (4) multi-agent coordination failures, (5) safety and security failures under adversarial or underspecified conditions, and (6) measurement validity problems. The taxonomy was derived iteratively by grouping independently reported error categories into themes corresponding to distinct stages of the agent reasoning-to-action pipeline. Across the literature, we find that failures compound nonlinearly with task length, that strong performance on individual sub-tasks does not reliably translate into end-to-end success, and that additional scaffolding does not consistently improve reliability.
